## 9. Wave-33 day-two reading and reruns (DX16)

The day2 card stays within 8,000 UTF-8 bytes and walks save-v1, change-specific
routes, preview, v2 deploy, rollback, forward, frozen callers and production
accounts. Init adaptation prints it first; a preview does not record reading
history. Routes ask for at most two further sections. The card says which
references to leave closed. Large reference cards flatten into independently
bounded sections at paragraph/fence boundaries; --full lists them rather than
bypassing the budget. Selection uses the existing project-local content hashes;
--repeat alone forces a selected reread. An oversized executable account example
is a packed reference file behind a pointer, not an exception to the budget.
The compiled source corpus retains complete examples. Publication extractors
explicitly iterate bounded sections when they need the full reference corpus.

Before edits, init --adapt --day2 --apply captures v1/ source modules and its
checked generated API under its own type:module package. It never overwrites
an existing v1 directory or application-owned calls/proofs. A missing day-one
client:calls remains an explicit coverage gap. The frozen runner accepts inline
CALLS_USERS or an existing file. Inline JSON becomes a private temporary file
for legacy file-only clients, removed after execution; credentials never enter
the saved source. Package dependencies resolve from the parent installation.
Historical API handles stay frozen when working sources are regenerated.

uniqueName creates row/intent names; freshUser uses real signup and ordinary
operator provisioning on development, staging or production proof hosts. It
creates a fresh identity rather than altering the persistent production roster.
One proof reuses its sessions across release cycles. Capacity advice on proof
CLI/provision failures and the generated business hook preserves refusal codes,
never retries writes, and recommends fresh owners/groups or deliberate cleanup.
Changing limits or resetting shared production is not a proof repair.
The proof digest derives exact declarations and includes production account and
private roster shapes, so builders need not read proof.js.

Administrative data --all and snapshotAll use a read-only retained
inspection adapter in one snapshot. They merge active rows, retained rows and
retired cells once per ID, retaining explicit nulls and preferring active cells
when representations overlap. Tagged historical values are decoded in their own
stored representation, not projected through the current column type. Cell-only
residue cannot resurrect a missing row. Deleted rows remain deleted. Ordinary limited
data keeps its active-only view; ephemeral tables refuse disk snapshots.
This changes presentation, not release storage or application read authority.
Releases --json remains the separate retained-source history inspection.

Evidence records named wave-33 lane regressions, packed consumer proofs and four
paired real-model day-two reading replays. Reading replays state their stopping
milestone and do not confer full-application or publication qualification.
Serve lifetime, release activation, security gates and latency stay with their
parallel owners. No package version bump, push or Mac qualification is made.
//! A day-one headless client must keep its own handles and module boundary.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — capture before editing; never overwrite historical evidence.
use std::{path::Path, io};
use serde_json::json;

pub(super) fn files(dir: &Path, sources: &[(String, String)]) -> io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    if dir.join("v1").symlink_metadata().is_ok() { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err(io::Error::other("cannot freeze an unchecked v1 client; fix current schema first")); }
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    fn push(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>, path: String, text: String) {
        if let Some((_,body))=files.iter_mut().find(|(name,_)|*name==path) { *body=text; }
        else { files.push((Box::leak(path.into_boxed_str()),text)); }
    }
    fn walk(root: &Path, relative: &Path, files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>) -> io::Result<()> {
        let path = root.join(relative);
        if !path.exists() { return Ok(()); }
        if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() { return Err(io::Error::other(format!("frozen client refuses symlink {}", path.display()))); }
        if path.is_dir() {
            if matches!(path.file_name().and_then(|n|n.to_str()),Some("node_modules"|"dist"|"target"|".git")) { return Ok(()); }
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(path)? { walk(root, &relative.join(entry?.file_name()),files)?; }
        } else if matches!(path.extension().and_then(|s|s.to_str()), Some("mjs"|"cjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"|"jsx")) || path.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="package.json") {
            push(files,format!("v1/{}",relative.display()),std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    // Save application modules, not node_modules, stores, credentials or build output.
    for path in ["scripts", "src", "app"] { walk(dir,Path::new(path),&mut files)?; }
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).into_iter().flatten() {
        let entry=entry?;
        if entry.path().is_file() && entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|e|matches!(e.to_str(),Some("mjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"))) { walk(dir,Path::new(&entry.file_name()),&mut files)?; }
    }
    // Follow literal relative module imports beyond conventional source directories.
    // Runtime-computed paths remain application responsibilities, as in Node itself.
    let canonical_root=dir.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| std::env::current_dir().unwrap_or_default().join(dir));
    let mut index=0;
    while index<files.len() {
        let (saved,text)=&files[index];
        let source=dir.join(saved.strip_prefix("v1/").unwrap());
        let mut imports=Vec::new();
        let bytes=text.as_bytes();let mut cursor=0;
        while cursor<bytes.len() {
            let quote=bytes[cursor];
            if !matches!(quote,b'\''|b'"') {cursor+=1;continue;}
            let start=cursor+1;let mut end=start;
            while end<bytes.len() && bytes[end]!=quote {if bytes[end]==b'\\' {end+=1;}end+=1;}
            if end>=bytes.len() {break;}
            let before=text[..cursor].trim_end();
            let module=before.ends_with("from")||before.ends_with("import")||before.ends_with("import(")||before.ends_with("require(");
            if module {if let Some(value)=text.get(start..end) {if value.starts_with("./")||value.starts_with("../") {imports.push(value.to_owned());}}}
            cursor=end+1;
        }
        for import in imports {
            let path=source.parent().unwrap().join(import);
            if !path.exists() {continue;}
            let canonical=path.canonicalize()?;
            let relative=canonical.strip_prefix(&canonical_root).map_err(|_|io::Error::other("frozen relative import escapes the app; keep client modules inside the project"))?;
            let saved=format!("v1/{}",relative.display());
            if files.iter().any(|(name,_)|*name==saved) {continue;}
            if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() {return Err(io::Error::other("frozen module imports must not use symlinks"));}
            if path.is_file() {push(&mut files,saved,std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);}
        }
        index+=1;
    }
    for (name,text) in sources { push(&mut files,format!("v1/snapback/{name}"),text.clone()); }
    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/api.ts".into(),snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound));
    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/package.json".into(),"{\"type\":\"module\"}\n".into());
    let package: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
    let command=package["scripts"]["client:calls"].as_str();
    let mut frozen=json!({"private":true,"type":"module","scripts":{"client:calls":"node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs"}});
    if package["scripts"].is_object() { frozen["scripts"]=package["scripts"].clone(); }
    frozen["scripts"]["client:calls"]=json!("node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs");
    if let Some(command)=command { frozen["scripts"]["client:calls:frozen"]=json!(command); }
    for group in ["dependencies","devDependencies"] { if package[group].is_object() { frozen[group]=package[group].clone(); } }
    push(&mut files,"v1/package.json".into(),super::pretty(&frozen));
    push(&mut files,"v1/.snapback4-frozen-run.mjs".into(),include_str!("frozen-run.mjs").into());
    Ok(files)
}
// API signatures: snapback4 guide api proof | client | auth | generated
// Frozen source uses its saved API. Inline users are adapted for legacy file readers.
import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join, dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
const cwd = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const pkg = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(cwd, "package.json"), "utf8"));
if (!pkg.scripts?.["client:calls:frozen"]) throw Error("No day-one client:calls was present at capture; add a business client before claiming frozen-client coverage.");
let temporary;
const env = { ...process.env };
try {
  if (env.CALLS_USERS?.trimStart().startsWith("{")) {
    const users = JSON.parse(env.CALLS_USERS);
    if (!users || Array.isArray(users) || Object.values(users).some(batch => !Array.isArray(batch))) throw Error("CALLS_USERS must be a role-to-account-arrays object");
    temporary = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "snapback4-v1-users-"));
    env.CALLS_USERS = join(temporary, "users.json");
    await writeFile(env.CALLS_USERS, JSON.stringify(users), {mode:0o600});
  }
  if (env.CALLS_USERS && !temporary) env.CALLS_USERS = resolve(process.env.INIT_CWD ?? process.cwd(), env.CALLS_USERS);
  const child = spawn("npm", ["run", "--silent", "client:calls:frozen"], {cwd,env,stdio:"inherit"});
  const signals = ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"];
  const forward = signal => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch {} };
  const handlers = signals.map(signal => {const fn=()=>forward(signal);process.on(signal,fn);return fn;});
  try {
    const result = await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{child.once("error",reject);child.once("exit",(code,signal)=>resolve({code,signal}));});
    process.exitCode = result.code ?? 1;
  } finally { signals.forEach((signal,i)=>process.off(signal,handlers[i])); }
} finally { if (temporary) await rm(temporary,{recursive:true,force:true}); }

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/inspect_merged.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/inspect_merged.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..e1bf62bf13
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/inspect_merged.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+//! Operator snapshots preserve tagged values even when historical column types differ.
+// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — merge current and retained images once per id, never via the active SQL scalar type.
+use super::{Inspection, Result, store_error};
+use crate::{Value, schema::{Schema, materialize_optional_fields},inspection::{InspectionCatalog,internal}};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+impl Inspection {
+    pub fn merged_records(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
+        if internal(name) {return Err(("E_SQL_INTERNAL","data reads application tables only".into()));}
+        let (_,encoded)=self.active_schema_record()?;
+        let active:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&encoded).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
+        active.refuse_private(name).map_err(|e|("E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",e.to_string()))?;
+        let schema=self.retained_schema(active.clone())?;
+        schema.refuse_private(name).map_err(|e|("E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",e.to_string()))?;
+        let definition=schema.table(name).ok_or_else(||("E_SCHEMA",format!("no table {name}")))?;
+        if definition.ephemeral.is_some() {return Err(("E_SCHEMA",format!("data {name}: ephemeral rows live only in serving memory; no disk snapshot")));}
+        let number:i64=self.conn.query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1",[name],|row|row.get(0)).map_err(store_error)?;
+        let mut merged=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeMap<String,Value>>::new();
+        // Active images win, including explicit null; retained cells fill only absent names.
+        let mut query=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id,data,0 AS rank FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data,1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data,2 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY rank,id").map_err(store_error)?;
+        let rows=query.query_map([number],|row|Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?,row.get::<_,String>(1)?,row.get::<_,i64>(2)?))).map_err(store_error)?;
+        for row in rows {
+            let (id,data,rank)=row.map_err(store_error)?;
+            let mut value:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
+            if rank==0 {if let Some(table)=active.table(name) {materialize_optional_fields(&mut value,table);}}
+            let Value::Row(fields)=value else {return Err(("E_SCHEMA","inspection row has no row image".into()));};
+            let target=merged.entry(id.clone()).or_default();
+            target.entry("id".into()).or_insert(Value::Id(id));
+            for (column,value) in fields.iter() {
+                if definition.columns.contains_key(column) {target.entry(column.clone()).or_insert_with(||value.clone());}
+            }
+        }
+        let mut query=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id,column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id,column_name").map_err(store_error)?;
+        let cells=query.query_map([number],|row|Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?,row.get::<_,String>(1)?,row.get::<_,String>(2)?))).map_err(store_error)?;
+        for cell in cells {
+            let (id,column,data)=cell.map_err(store_error)?;
+            // Cell-only residue cannot create a row that no retained image owns.
+            if let Some(target)=merged.get_mut(&id) {
+                if definition.columns.contains_key(&column) {
+                    let value:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
+                    target.entry(column).or_insert(value);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(merged.into_values().map(|fields|Value::Row(fields.into())).collect())
+    }
+}

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/data_all.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/data_all.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..9a3f895a68
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/data_all.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+//! Complete operator inspection over one read-only retained snapshot.
+// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — retained cells supplement active images once per id.
+use super::Options;
+use snapback4_core::store_port::AnyInspection;
+use serde_json::json;
+use std::path::Path;
+pub(super) fn run(path: &Path, table: &str, options: &Options) -> i32 {
+    let inspected=(|| {
+        let conn=AnyInspection::open(path)?;
+        conn.begin_snapshot()?;
+        conn.merged_records(table)
+    })();
+    match inspected {
+        Ok(rows) => {
+            let rows:Vec<_>=rows.iter().map(snapback4_core::wire::to_json).collect();
+            if options.json { println!("{}",json!({"rows":rows,"more":false,"limit":null,"inspection":"active and retained records, merged by id"})); }
+            else { for row in rows {println!("{row}");} }
+            0
+        }
+        Err((code,message)) => { eprintln!("{code}: {message}"); 1 }
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..55b3fcf39d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/pages.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,59 @@
+//! Bounded reading sections; the compiled corpus retains complete examples.
+// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — explicit reference reads use the same byte budget.
+use super::{FULL_BYTES, reading_blocks};
+
+/// Flatten nested reference headings and split at complete paragraphs/fences.
+/// Long runnable references stay in the package, behind a file pointer.
+pub fn reader_card(name: &str, card: &str) -> String {
+    if card.len() <= FULL_BYTES { return card.into(); }
+    let title = card.lines().next().unwrap_or("# Guide");
+    let room = FULL_BYTES.saturating_sub(title.len() + 350);
+    let mut out = format!("{title}\n\nRead one section for your change. `guide {name} --full` lists sections; --repeat reprints a selection.\n\n");
+    let mut heading = "Opening".to_owned();
+    let mut page = String::new();
+    let mut part = 1;
+    let flush = |out: &mut String, page: &mut String, heading: &str, part: usize| {
+        if page.is_empty() { return; }
+        out.push_str(&format!("## {heading}{}\n\n{}\n", if part == 1 { String::new() } else { format!(" (continued {part})") }, page));
+        page.clear();
+    };
+    let mut fenced = false;
+    // Isolate headings even where source prose has no preceding blank line.
+    let mut prepared = String::new();
+    for line in card.lines().skip(1) {
+        if line.starts_with("```") {
+            if !fenced { prepared.push('\n'); }
+            fenced = !fenced;
+        }
+        if !fenced && (line.starts_with("## ") || line.starts_with("### ")) { prepared.push('\n'); }
+        prepared.push_str(line); prepared.push('\n');
+        if !fenced && (line.starts_with("## ") || line.starts_with("### ") || line.starts_with("```")) { prepared.push('\n'); }
+    }
+    for block in reading_blocks(&prepared) {
+        if block.starts_with("## ") || block.starts_with("### ") {
+            flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part);
+            heading = block.trim().trim_start_matches('#').trim().to_owned(); part = 1;
+            continue;
+        }
+        let block = if block.len() > room && block.starts_with("```") {
+            // This exact executable reference is packed by pack-recipes.mjs.
+            assert!(name == "testing" && block.contains("day2-accounts"), "oversized guide fence needs its own packed reference: {name}");
+            format!("Complete runnable reference: node_modules/snapback4/dist/recipes/day2-accounts.mjs. Run it with Node; read it only when adapting that account-cycle example.\n")
+        } else { block };
+        // Dense prose can exceed a section; split only prose at word boundaries.
+        let chunks = if block.len() > room {
+            let mut chunks = Vec::new(); let mut chunk = String::new();
+            for word in block.split_inclusive(char::is_whitespace) {
+                if chunk.len() + word.len() > room && !chunk.is_empty() { chunks.push(std::mem::take(&mut chunk)); }
+                chunk.push_str(word);
+            }
+            if !chunk.is_empty() { chunks.push(chunk); } chunks
+        } else { vec![block] };
+        for chunk in chunks {
+            if page.len() + chunk.len() + 1 > room { flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part); part += 1; }
+            page.push_str(&chunk); page.push('\n');
+        }
+    }
+    flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part);
+    out
+}

+/** Real signup and ordinary role provisioning, with fresh credentials on each call.
+ * Reuse the returned session within one proof's release cycles. No persistent roster.
+ * @throws the same auth/provision/environment refusals as account setup; partial accounts may exist. */
+export async function freshUser(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: FreshUserOptions = {}): Promise<StagingAccount> {
+  if (options.role !== undefined && !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(options.role)) throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: freshUser role must name a role");
+  validatePlan({...options,...(options.role ? {roles:{[options.role]:1}} : {users:1})});
+  const response = await fetch(host.url + "/auth/config", {signal:AbortSignal.timeout(options.timeoutMs ?? 30_000)});
+  const config = await response.json() as {environment?: string};
+  const environment = config.environment;
+  if (!response.ok || !["development","staging","production"].includes(environment ?? "")) throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: freshUser requires a password proof host");
+  const sessions = await accounts(host, {
+    ...(options.role ? {roles:{[options.role]:1}} : {users:1}),
+    ...(options.provision ? {provision:options.provision} : {}),
+    ...(options.service ? {service:options.service} : {}),
+    ...(options.args ? {args:options.args} : {}),
+    ...(options.timeoutMs === undefined ? {} : {timeoutMs:options.timeoutMs}),
+  }, environment as "development" | "staging" | "production");
+  return sessions[options.role ?? "users"]![0]!;
+}
//! Bounded reading sections; the compiled corpus retains complete examples.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — explicit reference reads use the same byte budget.
use super::{FULL_BYTES, reading_blocks};

/// Flatten nested reference headings and split at complete paragraphs/fences.
/// Long runnable references stay in the package, behind a file pointer.
pub fn reader_card(name: &str, card: &str) -> String {
    if card.len() <= FULL_BYTES { return card.into(); }
    let title = card.lines().next().unwrap_or("# Guide");
    let room = FULL_BYTES.saturating_sub(title.len() + 350);
    let mut out = format!("{title}\n\nRead one section for your change. `guide {name} --full` lists sections; --repeat reprints a selection.\n\n");
    let mut heading = "Opening".to_owned();
    let mut page = String::new();
    let mut part = 1;
    let flush = |out: &mut String, page: &mut String, heading: &str, part: usize| {
        if page.is_empty() { return; }
        out.push_str(&format!("## {heading}{}\n\n{}\n", if part == 1 { String::new() } else { format!(" (continued {part})") }, page));
        page.clear();
    };
    let mut fenced = false;
    // Isolate headings even where source prose has no preceding blank line.
    let mut prepared = String::new();
    for line in card.lines().skip(1) {
        if line.starts_with("```") {
            if !fenced { prepared.push('\n'); }
            fenced = !fenced;
        }
        if !fenced && (line.starts_with("## ") || line.starts_with("### ")) { prepared.push('\n'); }
        prepared.push_str(line); prepared.push('\n');
        if !fenced && (line.starts_with("## ") || line.starts_with("### ") || line.starts_with("```")) { prepared.push('\n'); }
    }
    for block in reading_blocks(&prepared) {
        if block.starts_with("## ") || block.starts_with("### ") {
            flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part);
            heading = block.trim().trim_start_matches('#').trim().to_owned(); part = 1;
            continue;
        }
        let block = if block.len() > room && block.starts_with("```") {
            // This exact executable reference is packed by pack-recipes.mjs.
            assert!(name == "testing" && block.contains("day2-accounts"), "oversized guide fence needs its own packed reference: {name}");
            format!("Complete runnable reference: node_modules/snapback4/dist/recipes/day2-accounts.mjs. Run it with Node; read it only when adapting that account-cycle example.\n")
        } else { block };
        // Dense prose can exceed a section; split only prose at word boundaries.
        let chunks = if block.len() > room {
            let mut chunks = Vec::new(); let mut chunk = String::new();
            for word in block.split_inclusive(char::is_whitespace) {
                if chunk.len() + word.len() > room && !chunk.is_empty() { chunks.push(std::mem::take(&mut chunk)); }
                chunk.push_str(word);
            }
            if !chunk.is_empty() { chunks.push(chunk); } chunks
        } else { vec![block] };
        for chunk in chunks {
            if page.len() + chunk.len() + 1 > room { flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part); part += 1; }
            page.push_str(&chunk); page.push('\n');
        }
    }
    flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part);
    out
}
//! Complete operator inspection over one read-only retained snapshot.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — retained cells supplement active images once per id.
use super::Options;
use snapback4_core::store_port::AnyInspection;
use serde_json::json;
use std::path::Path;
pub(super) fn run(path: &Path, table: &str, options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let inspected=(|| {
        let conn=AnyInspection::open(path)?;
        conn.begin_snapshot()?;
        conn.merged_records(table)
    })();
    match inspected {
        Ok(rows) => {
            let rows:Vec<_>=rows.iter().map(snapback4_core::wire::to_json).collect();
            if options.json { println!("{}",json!({"rows":rows,"more":false,"limit":null,"inspection":"active and retained records, merged by id"})); }
            else { for row in rows {println!("{row}");} }
            0
        }
        Err((code,message)) => { eprintln!("{code}: {message}"); 1 }
    }
}
