//! Starter leftovers on the init-first path: `init --web` (or `--expo`) wrote
//! the todo starter, then the project replaced `snapback/schema.q`. The
//! starter's own files that still match the bytes init wrote, but no longer
//! fit the schema, are leftovers: `check` names them and `init --adapt`
//! removes or rewrites them. A file the project changed is never touched.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — leftovers are judged by the starter's original bytes, never by name alone.
use super::starter::{self, Body, Platform};
use super::{OwnSchema, own_schema};
use std::path::Path;

/// One unmodified starter file that no longer fits the project's schema.
pub(crate) struct Leftover {
    pub path: &'static str,
    pub why: String,
    /// The text `init --adapt` writes; `None` removes the file.
    pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

/// Starter operations its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
const STARTER_OPERATIONS: [&str; 4] = ["list", "add", "toggle", "remove"];

/// Whether a schema still has the starter's `todos` table and the operations
/// its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
pub(super) fn fits_todos(own: &OwnSchema) -> bool {
    own.tables.iter().any(|table| table == "todos")
        && STARTER_OPERATIONS.iter().all(|op| own.operations.iter().any(|name| name == op))
}

/// What the schema in `dir` leaves of the starter, and the advice that goes
/// with it (a sign-in the project must write by hand). No schema: nothing.
pub(crate) fn leftovers(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>) {
    let (found, advice, _) = plan(dir);
    (found, advice)
}

fn plan(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let Ok(Some(own)) = own_schema(dir) else { return (Vec::new(), Vec::new(), Vec::new()) };
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    let mut modified = Vec::new();
    let todos = fits_todos(&own);
    for (path, text) in starter::FIXTURES {
        let current = read(dir, path);
        if current.as_ref().is_some_and(|current| *current != super::without_design_references(text)) { modified.push(path.to_owned()); }
        if !todos && current.is_some_and(|current| current == super::without_design_references(text)) {
            let what = if path.ends_with("todos.jsonl") { "rows for the todo starter's `todos` table" } else { "cases for the todo starter's list/add/toggle/remove" };
            found.push(Leftover { path, why: format!("the unmodified {what}; your snapback/*.q has no todos table with those operations, so `snapback4 test` would refuse it"), rewrite: None });
        }
    }
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        if !screens(dir, platform, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut advice) {
            for path in [platform.paths().0, platform.paths().1] { if read(dir, path).is_some() { modified.push(path.to_owned()); } }
        }
    }
    if let Some(text) = read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts") {
        let wanted = starter::native_smoke(own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests));
        let recognized = if std::fs::symlink_metadata(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts")).is_ok_and(|m| m.file_type().is_symlink()) { None } else { super::proof_advice::native_template(&text) };
        if recognized.is_none() { modified.push("src/native-smoke.ts".to_owned()); }
        if recognized.is_some() && !smoke_referenced(dir, &found) {
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why: "the unmodified helper is now supplied by snapback4-expo/native-smoke; the planned screens no longer import this copy".into(), rewrite: None });
        } else if text != wanted && let Some((version, password)) = recognized && smoke_referenced(dir, &found) {
            let old = text != starter::native_smoke(password);
            let why = if old {
                let mode = if password != own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests) { "; refresh also updates its sign-in mode" } else { "" };
                format!("an older starter version ({version}; adapt can refresh it){mode}")
            } else {
                "the unmodified native smoke helper uses a different starter sign-in mode (adapt can refresh it)".into()
            };
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
        }
    }
    if read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts").is_some() && !smoke_referenced(dir, &found) && !found.iter().any(|f| f.path == "src/native-smoke.ts" && f.rewrite.is_none()) {
        advice.push("info: src/native-smoke.ts has no source reference; review it before a native smoke proof".into());
    }
    if !todos && let Some(web) = [false, true].into_iter().find(|web| super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, *web).is_some_and(|(_, template)| template.is_none())) {
        found.push(Leftover { path: "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", why: "the unmodified todo proof needs your screen's write assertions; keep a runnable frontend scaffold that explicitly reports the missing scenario".into(), rewrite: Some(super::proof::frontend_scaffold(web)) });
    }
    if found.iter().any(|change| change.rewrite.as_ref().is_some_and(|text| text.contains("/shared.ts\""))) {
        for (path, source) in [("src/shared.ts", include_str!("starter/shared.ts")), ("src/package.json", "{\"type\":\"module\"}\n")] {
            if !dir.join(path).exists() { found.push(Leftover { path, why: "shared TypeScript imported by the starter and Node proof".into(), rewrite: Some(source.into()) }); }
        }
    }
    if let Some(text) = read(dir, ".gitignore") {
        let revised = super::starter_gitignore(&text);
        if revised != text { found.push(Leftover { path: ".gitignore", why: "the starter's generated runtime imports must survive a git copy".into(), rewrite: Some(revised) }); }
    }
    readme(dir, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut modified);
    app_json(dir, &mut found);
    expo_favicon(dir, &mut found);
    (found, advice, modified)
}

// Default proof upgrades share the screen's custody decision. Read planned
// helper bytes so a proof cannot acquire imports or controls its kept app lacks.
fn default_frontend_ready(dir: &Path, changes: &[Leftover], modified: &[String]) -> bool {
    let app_path = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" };
    if read(dir, app_path).is_none() || read(dir, "src/client.ts").is_none()
        || modified.iter().any(|path| path == app_path || path == "src/client.ts") { return false; }
    [("src/shared.ts", include_str!("starter/shared.ts")), ("src/package.json", "{\"type\":\"module\"}\n")].into_iter().all(|(path, expected)| {
        if let Some(change) = changes.iter().find(|change| change.path == path) {
            change.rewrite.as_deref() == Some(expected)
        } else { read(dir, path).as_deref() == Some(expected) }
    })
}

// A custom entry/component/proof can reference the helper too. Generated trees
// and dependency directories are not authored source; do not follow symlinks.
fn smoke_referenced(root: &Path, changes: &[Leftover]) -> bool {
    fn walk(root: &Path, dir: &Path, helper: &Path, changes: &[Leftover]) -> bool {
        let Ok(entries)=std::fs::read_dir(dir) else { return true; };
        for entry in entries {
            let Ok(entry)=entry else {return true;};
            let Ok(kind)=entry.file_type() else {return true;};
            let path=entry.path();
            if kind.is_symlink() {
                if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(),Some("node_modules"|".git"|".snapback4"|".expo"|"target"|"dist"|"build"|"generated")) {continue;}
                return true; // Unknown source may refer back to the local helper.
            }
            if kind.is_dir() {
                if !matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(),Some("node_modules"|".git"|".snapback4"|".expo"|"target"|"dist"|"build"|"generated")) && walk(root,&path,helper,changes) {return true;}
            } else if path!=helper && !path.to_string_lossy().ends_with(".d.ts")
                && path.extension().and_then(|e|e.to_str()).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,"ts"|"tsx"|"js"|"jsx"|"mjs"|"cjs")) {
                let relative = path.strip_prefix(root).unwrap().to_string_lossy();
                let text = if let Some(change) = changes.iter().find(|f| f.path == relative) {
                    let Some(text) = &change.rewrite else { continue; }; Some(text.clone())
                } else { std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok() };
                if text.is_none_or(|text| {
                    let text = text.replace("snapback4-expo/native-smoke", "");
                    if text.contains("/native-smoke") { return true; }
                    let compact: String = text.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace()).collect();
                    if ["from\"native-smoke\"", "from'native-smoke'", "import\"native-smoke\"", "import'native-smoke'"].iter().any(|s| compact.contains(*s)) { return true; }
                    // A CLI mode named native-smoke is not a module reference.
                    // Unknown dynamic imports/requires keep the local helper.
                    ["import(", "require("].iter().any(|call| compact.match_indices(*call).any(|(at, _)| {
                        let argument = &compact[at + call.len()..];
                        !argument.starts_with(['\'', '"']) || argument.starts_with("'native-smoke'") || argument.starts_with("\"native-smoke\"")
                    }))
                }) {return true;}
            }
        }
        false
    }
    walk(root,root,&root.join("src/native-smoke.ts"),changes)
}

/// The starter README, while it is still one init wrote (for either screen,
/// at the ports it names), when it describes another screen than the schema
/// now gets: the Todos README beside a project's schema, or the reverse.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): adapt rewrote the screen but left a README about todos and guest sign-in.
fn readme(dir: &Path, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, modified: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let platform = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Web } else if dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Expo } else { return };
    let title = super::title(dir);
    let personas = own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty();
    let wanted_body = if todos { Body::Todos } else { Body::Home(None) };
    for path in ["README.md", "README.snapback.md"] {
        let Some(text) = read(dir, path) else { continue };
        let port = number_after(&text, "127.0.0.1:");
        let web_port = number_after(&text, "localhost:");
        let ports = [(port, web_port), (None, web_port), (port, None), (None, None)];
        // The offline paragraph names the schema's synced tables when init
        // wrote it; any of them (or 0.4.22's unconditional claim) still matches.
        let masked = starter::without_offline(&text);
        let explicit_online = platform == Platform::Web && read(dir, "src/client.ts").is_some_and(|client| client.contains("writes: \"online\""));
        let matched = ports.into_iter().find(|&(port, web_port)| {
            [(Body::Todos, false), (Body::Home(None), false), (Body::Home(None), true)].iter()
                .any(|(body, personas)| [false, true].iter().any(|password| [false, true].iter().any(|online| {
                    let readme = starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, body, *personas, &starter::Offline::Unknown, *password);
                    let readme = if *online { starter::online_readme(&readme) } else { readme };
                    masked == starter::without_offline(&super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&readme), port, web_port))
                })))
        });
        let Some((port, web_port)) = matched else { modified.push(path.to_owned()); continue };
        let wanted = super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, &wanted_body, personas, &own.offline, own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests))), port, web_port);
        let wanted = if explicit_online { starter::online_readme(&wanted) } else { wanted };
        let wanted = if todos { super::proof::readme(wanted) } else { wanted };
        let wanted = if dir.join("scripts/install.mjs").is_file() {wanted.replace("npm install", "node scripts/install.mjs")} else {wanted};
        if wanted == text { continue; }
        let about = if todos { "the todo starter's operations, which your snapback/*.q declares again" } else { "your snapback/*.q and the Home screen, named after this directory" };
        let only_proof = super::proof::without_readme(&wanted) == text;
        let only_offline = !text.starts_with("# Todos") && starter::without_offline(&wanted) == masked;
        // A schema that does not compile right now says nothing about what it syncs.
        if only_offline && !only_proof && own.offline == starter::Offline::Unknown { continue; }
        let why = if only_proof {
            "the unmodified starter README lacks frontend/day-two proof instructions; adapt adds them".to_owned()
        } else if only_offline {
            "the unmodified starter README says what works offline for another schema; adapt rewrites that paragraph from the tables your snapback/*.q syncs".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("the unmodified starter README describes {}; adapt rewrites it to describe {about}", if text.starts_with("# Todos") { "the todo starter (its todos table and guest sign-in)" } else { "the Home screen for another schema" })
        };
        found.push(Leftover { path, why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
    }
}

/// An Expo `app.json` that still carries the todo starter's slug, scheme and
/// bundle ids (written by 0.4.21 and earlier): adapt names them after the
/// directory and changes nothing else in the file.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (067): adapted Expo apps kept dev.snapback4.todos.
fn app_json(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    let expo = &config["expo"];
    let [(_, slug), (_, scheme), (_, bundle)] = starter::TODO_IDS;
    if expo["slug"] != slug || expo["scheme"] != scheme || expo["ios"]["bundleIdentifier"] != bundle || expo["android"]["package"] != bundle { return; }
    let (new_slug, new_scheme, new_bundle) = starter::app_ids(&super::title(dir));
    let quoted = |value: &str| format!("\"{value}\"");
    let rewrite = text.replace(&quoted(bundle), &quoted(&new_bundle)).replace(&quoted(slug), &quoted(&new_slug)).replace(&quoted(scheme), &quoted(&new_scheme));
    found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: format!("the todo starter's ids (slug {slug}, scheme {scheme}, bundle id {bundle}); adapt names them after this directory ({new_slug}, {new_scheme}, {new_bundle})"), rewrite: Some(rewrite) });
}

// Repair only the exact SVG asset and setting emitted by 0.4.26. Custom
// images/settings remain owned by the app, including other SVG paths.
fn expo_favicon(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    if read(dir, "assets/favicon.svg").as_deref() != Some(include_str!("starter/favicon.svg")) { return; }
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    if config.pointer("/expo/web/favicon").and_then(|value| value.as_str()) != Some("./assets/favicon.svg") { return; }
    let why = "the unmodified Expo favicon is SVG, which Expo's web export requires to be raster".to_owned();
    if let Some(existing) = found.iter_mut().find(|change| change.path == "app.json") {
        let mut config: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(existing.rewrite.as_ref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        existing.rewrite = Some(super::pretty(&config));
        existing.why.push_str("; Expo's web export also requires a raster favicon");
    } else {
        let mut config = config;
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: why.clone(), rewrite: Some(super::pretty(&config)) });
    }
    found.push(Leftover { path: "assets/favicon.svg", why, rewrite: None });
}

fn number_after(text: &str, prefix: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let at = text.find(prefix)? + prefix.len();
    let digits: String = text[at..].chars().take_while(char::is_ascii_digit).collect();
    digits.parse().ok()
}

/// The sign-in a schema admits, as the starter screen it needs: the guest
/// screen (`None`), the persona picker (`Some(personas)`), or no starter
/// screen fits (`Err(advice)`).
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum SignIn { Guest, Password, Personas(Vec<String>) }
impl SignIn {
    fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self { Self::Guest => "the guest sign-in".into(), Self::Password => "the password sign-in".into(), Self::Personas(names) => format!("a persona picker ({})", names.join(", ")) }
    }
    fn personas(&self) -> Option<&[String]> { match self { Self::Personas(names) => Some(names), _ => None } }
}
fn wanted(own: &OwnSchema) -> Result<SignIn, &'static str> {
    match own.identity {
        Some((_, true)) => Ok(SignIn::Guest),
        Some((true, false)) => Ok(SignIn::Password),
        None if !own.personas.is_empty() => Ok(SignIn::Personas(own.personas.clone())),
        _ => Err("declares neither `use identity` nor `use personas`, so nobody can act: add `use identity` for the guest sign-in or `use personas alice, bob` for development, then rerun `snapback4 init --adapt`"),
    }
}

fn screens(dir: &Path, platform: Platform, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, advice: &mut Vec<String>) -> bool {
    let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
    let (Some(app), Some(client)) = (read(dir, app_path), read(dir, client_path)) else { return true };
    // Which starter screen is on disk, if both files are still as init wrote
    // them: its sign-in (guest, or a persona picker) and its body.
    let journal_shape = starter::Offline::Tables { synced: vec![], total: 0 };
    let explicit_online = platform == Platform::Web && client.contains("writes: \"online\"");
    let client_shape = normalized(&client);
    let matches_client = |reference: &str, personas: bool| [reference, starter::previous_client(platform, personas)].iter().any(|reference|
        client_shape == normalized(reference) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::web_client((*reference).to_owned(), &journal_shape)) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::online_web_client((*reference).to_owned())) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::expo_client((*reference).to_owned(), &journal_shape)));
    let matches_app = |reference: &str| app == reference || app == reference.replace("export default function App", "export function App");
    let current = [Body::Todos, Body::Home(first_read(&app))].into_iter().find_map(|body| [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].into_iter().find_map(|version| {
        let screens = |personas: Option<&[String]>, password| match version { 5 => starter::dx13_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 1 => starter::previous_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 2 => starter::wave14_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 3 => starter::wave20_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 4 => starter::wave22_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), _ => starter::identity_screens(platform, personas, &body, password) };
        let (guest_app, guest_client) = screens(None, false);
        let (persona_app, persona_client) = screens(Some(&[]), false);
        let (password_app, password_client) = screens(None, true);
        if matches_app(&guest_app) && matches_client(&guest_client, false) { Some((SignIn::Guest, body.clone())) }
        else if matches_app(&password_app) && (matches_client(&password_client, false) || matches_client(starter::pre_dx13_password_client(platform), false)) { Some((SignIn::Password, body.clone())) }
        else if matches_app(&persona_app) && matches_client(&persona_client, true) { Some((SignIn::Personas(personas_of(&client)), body.clone())) }
        else { None }
    }));
    let Some((sign_in, body)) = current else { return false };
    // A Home keeps its read while the schema still declares that query
    // argument-free; a Home without one stays as written (it fits any schema).
    let wanted_body = match &body {
        _ if todos => Body::Todos,
        Body::Home(None) => Body::Home(None),
        Body::Home(Some(op)) if own.reads.contains(op) => body.clone(),
        _ => Body::Home(own.reads.first().cloned()),
    };
    // A sign-in no starter screen fits keeps the one on disk, with advice.
    let wanted_sign_in = match wanted(own) {
        Ok(wanted) => wanted,
        Err(why) => {
            advice.push(format!("{app_path} is the starter's {}, but your snapback/*.q {why}", sign_in.label()));
            sign_in.clone()
        }
    };
    let (app_text, mut client_text) = starter::identity_screens(platform, wanted_sign_in.personas(), &wanted_body, wanted_sign_in == SignIn::Password);
    if app_text.contains("/shared.ts\"") && dir.join("src/shared.ts").symlink_metadata().is_ok()
        && read(dir, "src/shared.ts").as_deref() != Some(include_str!("starter/shared.ts")) {
        advice.push(format!("{app_path}: keep your screen while src/shared.ts is application-owned; the refreshed starter needs its prepareTitle/matchesFilter exports"));
        return false;
    }
    client_text = if explicit_online { starter::online_web_client(client_text) } else if platform == Platform::Expo { starter::expo_client(client_text, &own.offline) } else { starter::web_client(client_text, &own.offline) };
    let client_changed = client_shape != normalized(&client_text);
    if platform == Platform::Expo && app_text.contains("export default function App") {
        let entry = include_str!("../../../../../apps/chat-expo/index.js");
        if read(dir,"index.js").is_some_and(|text| text == entry.replace("import App from", "import { App } from")) {
            found.push(Leftover { path: "index.js", why: "the owned Expo entry imports the old named App; the screen now exports App by default".into(), rewrite: Some(entry.into()) });
        }
    }
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && app == app_text && wanted_body == body && !client_changed { return true; }
    let mut reasons = Vec::new();
    let mut client_leftover = None;
    if wanted_sign_in != sign_in {
        let (from, to) = (sign_in.label(), wanted_sign_in.label());
        let because = match &wanted_sign_in { SignIn::Personas(_) => "your snapback/*.q declares `use personas` and no `use identity`", SignIn::Password => "your snapback/*.q declares `use identity password`", SignIn::Guest => "your snapback/*.q admits guests" };
        reasons.push(format!("the unmodified starter screen shows {from}, but {because}; adapt writes {to}"));
        // Keep the backend port init wrote.
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why: format!("the unmodified starter client for {from}; adapt writes {to}"), rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && client_changed {
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        let why = "the unmodified web client needs the device or principal journal selected by your schema's synced tables".to_owned();
        reasons.push(why.clone());
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why, rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_body != body {
        reasons.push(match (&wanted_body, &body) {
            (Body::Home(_), Body::Home(Some(op))) => format!("its Home screen reads api.{op}, which your snapback/*.q no longer declares as a query without required arguments; adapt writes the Home screen again{}", match &wanted_body { Body::Home(Some(next)) => format!(", reading api.{next}"), _ => String::new() }),
            (Body::Home(_), _) => "its Todos screen calls the starter's list/add/toggle/remove, which your snapback/*.q does not declare, so it would not typecheck; adapt writes a Home screen that compiles against any schema (the signed-in viewer, your operations from snapback/generated/api.ts, and your first query without required arguments rendered with matchRead) for you to replace".to_owned(),
            (Body::Todos, _) => "your snapback/*.q declares the todo starter's operations again; adapt writes the Todos screen back".to_owned(),
        });
    }
    let why = reasons.join("; ");
    let why = if why.starts_with("the unmodified") { why } else { format!("the unmodified starter screen: {why}") };
    found.push(Leftover { path: app_path, why, rewrite: Some(app_text) });
    found.extend(client_leftover);
    true
}

/// The `api` path a starter Home's first read calls, if it has one.
fn first_read(app: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let after = app.split_once("function FirstRead()")?.1;
    let op = after.split_once("useQuery(api.")?.1.split_once(',')?.0;
    Some(op.to_owned())
}

fn read(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let path = dir.join(path);
    path.symlink_metadata().ok().filter(|metadata| metadata.is_file())?;
    std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()
}

/// A starter client without what init or the schema varies: the backend port
/// and the persona list.
fn normalized(client: &str) -> String {
    let lines = client.split_inclusive('\n').map(|line| {
        if line.starts_with(PERSONAS) { format!("{PERSONAS}[];\n") } else { line.to_owned() }
    }).collect::<String>();
    match port_of(&lines) { Some(port) => lines.replace(&format!("127.0.0.1:{port}"), "127.0.0.1:4400"), None => lines }
}

const PERSONAS: &str = "export const personas: readonly string[] = ";

fn personas_of(client: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    client.lines().find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix(PERSONAS))
        .and_then(|list| serde_json::from_str(list.trim_end().trim_end_matches(';')).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn port_of(client: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let at = client.find("127.0.0.1:")? + "127.0.0.1:".len();
    let digits = client[at..].find(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(client.len() - at);
    (digits > 0).then(|| &client[at..at + digits])
}

/// One `check` line, or none: the leftovers and the command that fixes them.
pub(crate) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let (found, mut advice) = leftovers(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return None; }
    if found.is_empty() { return Some(advice.join("\n")); }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found { lines.push(format!("  {}: {}", leftover.path, leftover.why)); }
    for line in &advice { lines.push(format!("  {line}")); }
    if !found.is_empty() {
        lines.push("  fix: snapback4 init --adapt (removes or rewrites only these files, which still match the starter; your own files are never touched)".into());
    }
    Some(format!("info: starter: {} from `snapback4 init` to review\n{}",
        if found.is_empty() { "a screen".to_owned() } else { format!("{} file{}", found.len(), if found.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }) },
        lines.join("\n")))
}

/// The same, for `check --json`: `null` when nothing is left over.
pub(crate) fn report_json(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    let (found, mut advice) = leftovers(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return serde_json::Value::Null; }
    serde_json::json!({
        "files": found.iter().map(|leftover| serde_json::json!({ "path": leftover.path, "why": leftover.why, "adapt": if leftover.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" } })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        "advice": advice,
        "fix": if found.is_empty() { serde_json::Value::Null } else { "snapback4 init --adapt".into() },
    })
}

/// `snapback4 init --adapt [dir]`.
pub(super) fn adapt(dir: &Path, dry_run: bool, apply: bool, day2: bool) -> i32 {
    if day2 { println!("{}", if dry_run || !apply { snapback4_lang::guide::card("day2").unwrap().to_owned() } else { crate::guide_seen::render_in(dir, "day2", snapback4_lang::guide::card("day2").unwrap(), false) }); }
    let own = match own_schema(dir) {
        Ok(Some(own)) => own,
        Ok(None) => { eprintln!("init --adapt: no snapback/*.q in {}; nothing to adapt to (run `snapback4 init --web` or `--expo` first)", dir.display()); return 1; }
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let (found, mut advice, modified) = plan(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
    let mut proof = match super::proof::plan(dir, &own, day2, default_frontend_ready(dir, &found, &modified)) {
        Ok(proof) => proof,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    if day2 {
        let sources = own.files.iter().map(|name| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("snapback").join(name)).map(|text|(name.clone(),text))).collect::<std::io::Result<Vec<_>>>();
        match sources.and_then(|sources|super::frozen::files(dir,&sources)) {
            Ok(files) => proof.extend(files.into_iter().filter(|(name,_)| !proof.iter().any(|(existing,_)|existing==name)).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("init --adapt frozen client: {error}"); return 2; }
        }
    }
    if !found.is_empty() || !proof.is_empty() {
        println!("init --adapt: planned changes (only unmodified starter files):");
        for change in &found { println!("  {} {} — {}", if change.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" }, change.path, change.why); }
    }
    for line in super::proof_advice::legacy_differences(dir, &proof) { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
    let frozen_count=proof.iter().filter(|(path,_)|path.starts_with("v1/")).count();
    if frozen_count>0 { println!("  add v1/ — {frozen_count} frozen source/API/package files; ESM client:calls accepts inline or file CALLS_USERS"); }
    for (path, body) in &proof {
        if path.starts_with("v1/") {continue;}
        if path.starts_with("versions/") {
            println!("  add {path} — capture current snapback/*.q and contemporary fixtures; legacy flat sources stay unchanged");
            continue;
        }
        let exists = dir.join(path).exists();
        println!("  {} {} — {} proof infrastructure", if *path == "package.json" { "extend" } else if exists { "replace" } else { "add" }, path, if exists { "inherited" } else { "missing" });
        if *path == "package.json" {
            let old: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(path)).ok().and_then(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
            let new: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(body).unwrap();
            for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
                let keys = old[group].as_object().into_iter().flat_map(|o|o.keys()).chain(new[group].as_object().into_iter().flat_map(|o|o.keys())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
                for key in keys { if old[group][key] != new[group][key] { println!("    {group}.{key}: {} → {}", old[group][key], new[group][key]); } }
            }
            if old.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") != new.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") {
                println!("    snapback4.starter.day2: {} → {}", old["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"], new["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"]);
            }
            println!("    serialize package.json (including script key ordering)");
        }
    }
    if !modified.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: application-owned or modified files stay unchanged: {}", modified.join(", ")); }
    if dry_run || !apply {
        for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
        if !fits_todos(&own) && [false,true].into_iter().any(|web| super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).is_some_and(|(_,template)|template.is_none())) { println!("init --adapt: no write scenario: add an app-specific frontend proof with snapback4/proof after replacing Home"); }
        println!("init --adapt: preview only; no files changed. Apply with:");
        println!("snapback4 init{} --adapt{} --apply", if dir == Path::new(".") { String::new() } else { format!(" '{}'", dir.to_string_lossy().replace('\'', "'\"'\"'")) }, if day2 { " --day2" } else { "" });
        return 0;
    }
    if let Err(error) = super::proof::apply(dir, &proof) { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    let mut removed = Vec::new();
    let mut rewritten = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found {
        let path = dir.join(leftover.path);
        let result = match &leftover.rewrite {
            Some(text) => super::replace_file(&path, text).map(|()| rewritten.push(leftover.path)),
            None => std::fs::remove_file(&path).map(|()| removed.push(leftover.path)),
        };
        if let Err(error) = result { eprintln!("init --adapt: {}: {error}", path.display()); return 2; }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — adapt removes the files it names, never a directory: the next step writes fixtures/<table>.jsonl.
    if found.is_empty() && proof.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: nothing to adapt — no unmodified starter file conflicts with your snapback/*.q"); }
    if !removed.is_empty() {
        let kept = if removed.iter().any(|path| path.starts_with("fixtures/")) { "; fixtures/ stays for your own rows" } else { "" };
        println!("init --adapt: removed {} (unmodified starter files{kept}; write your own as fixtures/<table>.jsonl and fixtures/test.cases.jsonl, `snapback4 guide testing`)", removed.join(" and "));
    }
    if !rewritten.is_empty() {
        let why = found.iter().find(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.is_some()).map(|leftover| leftover.why.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
        println!("init --adapt: rewrote {} — {why}", rewritten.join(" and "));
    }
    if found.iter().any(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.as_deref().is_some_and(|text| text.contains("function Home("))) {
        println!("init --adapt: next: `snapback4 check` writes snapback/generated/api.ts for the Home screen; replace Home with your own screens (`snapback4 guide client`)");
    }
    for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
    0
}

#[test]
fn untouched_wave9_screens_still_adapt_and_edited_clients_stay_owned() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        for password in [false, true] {
            let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w10-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap(); std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
            let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q").replace("use identity ", "use identity password ");
            std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
            let (app, client) = starter::previous_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
            let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
            std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), &app).unwrap(); std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?}, password={password}: previous screens recognized");
            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path), "edited pair stays owned");
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
        }
    }
}


#[test]
fn untouched_wave14_screens_and_smoke_still_adapt() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w14-adapt-{}-{platform:?}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
        let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q").replace("use identity ", "use identity password ");
        std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
        let (app, client) = starter::wave14_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, false);
        assert!(!app.contains("No tasks yet."));
        let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), app).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts"), starter::wave14_native_smoke(false)).unwrap();
        let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
        assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path));
        if platform == Platform::Expo { assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == "src/native-smoke.ts")); }
        std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
        let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
        assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn old_native_smoke_distinguishes_starter_age_from_sign_in_changes() {
    for password in [false, true] {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-r2-{}-{password}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
        let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q");
        let schema = if password { schema.replace("use identity", "use identity password") } else { schema.into() };
        std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/custom.ts"), "import './native-smoke';").unwrap();
        for source in [include_str!("starter/previous/native-smoke-0.4.9.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/native-smoke-0.4.16.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave14/native-smoke.ts")] {
            for helper_password in [false,true] {
                std::fs::write(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts"), starter::native_smoke_version(helper_password, source)).unwrap();
                if helper_password && source == include_str!("starter/previous/native-smoke-0.4.9.ts") {
                    assert!(!leftovers(&dir).0.iter().any(|f|f.path=="src/native-smoke.ts"),"unshipped identity variants are not starter-owned");
                    continue;
                }
                let text = report(&dir).unwrap();
                assert!(text.contains("an older starter version") && !text.contains("previous schema") && !text.contains("not fit your schema"), "{text}");
                assert_eq!(text.contains("refresh also updates its sign-in mode"),helper_password!=password,"{text}");
            }
        }
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts"), starter::native_smoke(password)).unwrap();
        assert!(!leftovers(&dir).0.iter().any(|f|f.path=="src/native-smoke.ts"));
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts"), starter::native_smoke(!password)).unwrap();
        let current=report(&dir).unwrap();
        assert!(current.contains("different starter sign-in mode") && !current.contains("older starter") && !current.contains("previous schema"),"{current}");
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts"), format!("{}\n// mine", starter::wave14_native_smoke(password))).unwrap();
        assert!(!leftovers(&dir).0.iter().any(|f| f.path == "src/native-smoke.ts"));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn untouched_wave22_lists_and_clients_adapt_without_rewriting_owned_source() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        for password in [false, true] {
            let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w22-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
            let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q");
            let schema = if password { schema.replace("use identity ", "use identity password ") } else { schema.to_owned() };
            std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
            let (app, client) = starter::wave22_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
            let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
            std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), app).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?} password={password}: upgrade teaches ReadState");
            assert_eq!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == client_path), password || platform == Platform::Expo, "{platform:?} password={password}: password upgrades retain explicit witnesses; Expo omits absent optional device props");
            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path));
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn dx14_exact_dx13_screens_adapt_with_witness_registration_and_preserve_edits() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] { for password in [false, true] {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx14-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
        let source = include_str!("starter/schema.q");
        let source = if password { source.replace("use identity ", "use identity password ") } else { source.to_owned() };
        std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), source).unwrap();
        let (app, client) = starter::dx13_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
        let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), &app).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(".gitignore"), "# snapback4\n.snapback4/\nsnapback/generated/\nprivate/\n").unwrap();
        let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
        assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?} password={password}: DX13 screen is recognized");
        let resulting_client = changes.iter().find(|change| change.path == client_path).and_then(|change| change.rewrite.as_deref()).unwrap_or(&client);
        assert!(resulting_client.contains("registerWebWitness(app, api)"), "{platform:?} password={password}: {resulting_client}");
        let ignore = changes.iter().find(|change| change.path == ".gitignore").unwrap().rewrite.as_deref().unwrap();
        assert!(ignore.contains("private/") && !ignore.contains("snapback/generated/"));
        std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), format!("{app}\n// mine\n")).unwrap();
        let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
        assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path));
        std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), app).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("src/shared.ts"), "// application-owned helper\n").unwrap();
        let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
        assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path), "custom shared modules keep the screen owned");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }}
}

#[test]
fn historical_032_default_proof_refresh_requires_the_screen_and_shared_helpers() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] { for custody in ["authored-screen", "shared-collision", "missing-client", "refresh"] {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx14-proof-custody-{}-{platform:?}-{custody}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("scripts")).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), include_str!("starter/schema.q")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{"frontend:proof":"node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"}}"#).unwrap();
        let (mut app, client) = starter::dx13_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, false);
        if custody == "authored-screen" { app.push_str("\n// application-owned screen\n"); }
        let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), &app).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), client).unwrap();
        if custody == "shared-collision" { std::fs::write(dir.join("src/shared.ts"), "// application-owned helper\n").unwrap(); }
        if custody == "missing-client" {
            std::fs::remove_file(dir.join(client_path)).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(dir.join("src/shared.ts"), include_str!("starter/shared.ts")).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(dir.join("src/package.json"), "{\"type\":\"module\"}\n").unwrap();
        }
        let web = platform == Platform::Web;
        let old_proof = super::proof::frontend_proof(include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.32.mjs"), None, web);
        std::fs::write(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"), &old_proof).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(super::proof_advice::frontend_template(&dir, web), Some(("0.4.31" | "0.4.32", None))), "the actual historical proof must be recognized (0.4.31 and 0.4.32 share some bytes)");
        assert_eq!(adapt(&dir, false, true, false), 0);
        let proof = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")).unwrap();
        if custody == "refresh" {
            assert_ne!(proof, old_proof);
            assert!(proof.contains("../src/shared.ts"));
            assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("src/shared.ts")).unwrap(), include_str!("starter/shared.ts"));
            assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(app_path)).unwrap().contains("/shared.ts\""));
            assert!(proof.contains(if web { "combobox" } else { "Write status" }));
        } else {
            assert_eq!(proof, old_proof, "{platform:?} {custody}: keep the proof when its screen refresh is refused");
            assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(app_path)).unwrap(), app);
            if custody == "authored-screen" { assert!(!dir.join("src/shared.ts").exists()); }
            else if custody == "shared-collision" { assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("src/shared.ts")).unwrap(), "// application-owned helper\n"); }
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }}
}
import { capacityAdvice } from "./proof-fresh.ts";
import { rosterLock } from "./proof-roster-lock.ts";
import { identityRequest } from "./identity-request.ts";
import { randomUUID, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, unlinkSync, lstatSync, fsyncSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import type { ServeHost } from "./proof.ts";

export interface StagingAccount {
  kind: "user"; role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export type ProvisionedAccount = StagingAccount;
export type ProvisionedSessions = StagingSessions;
export interface ProvisionAccountsOptions extends SeedAccountsOptions {
  /** Named persistent roster within this deployment. Default default. */
  roster?: string;
  /** Required for a host not returned by startServe. Keep with the deployment. */
  rosterDir?: string;
}
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles?: Record<string, number>;
  provision?: { readonly name: string };
  /** Additional password users with no provision call; returned under sessions.users. */
  users?: number;
  /** Service seat authorized by the provisioning mutation. Default operator. */
  service?: string;
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {
  // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — custom argument keys override defaults; the seat grants no extra authority.
  const service = options.service ?? "operator";
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles ?? {}), users=options.users ?? 0, timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(users) || users<0 || (!roles.length&&!users) || (users>0&&roles.some(([role])=>role==="users")) || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, users) > 100 || (roles.length>0 || options.provision!==undefined) && (typeof options.provision?.name!=="string" || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision.name))) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  return {service, roles:[...roles,...(users ? [["users",users] as [string,number]] : [])], timeout};
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. Partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard the staging store before retrying. */
async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production" | "development", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}, assertHeld: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const partial = environment === "development" ? "inspect partial development accounts/grants or discard this development store" : environment === "staging" ? "inspect or discard this staging deployment" : "inspect partial production accounts/grants before retrying";
  const remaining = () => {
    assertHeld();
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
    return ms;
  };
  const guardedFetch: typeof fetch = (url, init) => { assertHeld(); return fetch(url, init); };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => guardedFetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== environment || identity.development !== (environment === "development") || !identity.password) {
    throw Error(environment === "development" ? "E_DEV_ACCOUNTS: devAccounts requires startDev with password identity; serve hosts refuse before creating users" : environment === "staging"
      ? "E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users"
      : "E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS: account provisioning requires serve on a production deploy with password identity; staging and dev refuse before creating users");
  }
  const operator = Object.keys(options.roles??{}).length ? await host.operatorToken(service) : "";
  if (environment === "production" && operator) {
    const response = await request("/schema", { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` } }), schema = await response.json();
    const named = response.ok && Array.isArray(schema.service_identities) && schema.service_identities.includes(`service:${service}`);
    if (!named) process.stderr.write(`snapback4: WARNING minted service:${service}, but the served schema reports no explicit positive authorization mention for that service identity; rules still decide every operation and provisionAccounts(${options.provision?.name}) will likely refuse.\n`);
  }
  const sessions: StagingSessions = Object.create(null);
  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let fresh = false;
      let entry = roster?.entries.find(entry => entry.role === role && entry.index === i);
      if (!entry) {
        fresh = true;
        entry = { role, index: i, email: `${environment}-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password: randomBytes(24).toString("base64url"), id: randomUUID(), done: false };
        roster?.entries.push(entry); save(); // Persist identity before the first possibly ambiguous write.
      }
      const { email, password } = entry;
      let reply: Record<string, unknown>;
      if (roster && !fresh) {
        const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/login", {
          method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
        }, guardedFetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
          if ((error as {code?:string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: login deadline expired; ${partial}`);
          remaining(); throw error;
        });
        reply = result.json;
        if (!reply.session && (entry.principal || (reply.denied as {code?:string})?.code !== "E_AUTH")) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: saved account login failed; inspect roster and deployment; no replacement account created");
      } else reply = {};
      if (!reply.session) {
      const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/signup", {
        method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
      }, guardedFetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
        if ((error as {code?: string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: signup deadline expired; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
        remaining(); throw error;
      });
      reply = result.json;
      }
      if ((reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code === "E_RATE_LIMIT") {
        throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: identity retry budget exhausted; increase timeoutMs or inspect partial accounts/grants before retrying");
      }
      const session = reply.session as {principal?:string;kind?:string;token?:string;expiresAt?:number} | undefined;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number" || session.kind !== "user") {
        throw Error(`E_STAGING_SIGNUP: signup refused (${(reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      }

//! Wave-33 day-two reading and frozen-client module failures.
use std::{fs,process::Command};
const BIN:&str=env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");
#[test]
fn wave33_029_024_011_023_033_frozen_client_has_own_esm_api_and_inline_users() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-freeze-{}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("scripts")).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),"use identity password\ntable notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'notes'\n  online only\nquery list():\n  return notes first 20 by .id\n").unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("package.json"),r#"{"scripts":{"client:calls":"node scripts/client-calls.mjs"}}"#).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("scripts/client-calls.mjs"),"import {readFileSync,statSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {api} from '../snapback/generated/api.ts';\nconst users=JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.env.CALLS_USERS));\nif(users.member[0].email!=='inline@example.test'||!api.list)throw Error('missing users or frozen handle');\nconsole.log(process.env.CALLS_USERS);\nconsole.log(statSync(process.env.CALLS_USERS).mode&511);\n").unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).env("SNAPBACK4_INIT_INSTALL","0").output().unwrap();
    assert!(run(&["check"]).status.success()); // ordinary checked day-one app already has an imported API
    let preview=run(&["init","--adapt","--day2"]);assert!(preview.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&preview.stderr));assert!(!dir.join("v1").exists());assert!(!dir.join(".snapback4/guide-seen.json").exists());
    let result=run(&["init","--adapt","--day2","--apply"]);assert!(result.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout).starts_with("# Day two:"));
    let package:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(dir.join("v1/package.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(package["type"],"module");
    let frozen=fs::read(dir.join("v1/snapback/generated/api.ts")).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),"table other:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'other'\n  online only\n").unwrap();
    assert!(run(&["init","--adapt","--day2","--apply"]).status.success());assert_eq!(fs::read(dir.join("v1/snapback/generated/api.ts")).unwrap(),frozen);
    let child=Command::new("npm").args(["--prefix","v1","run","--silent","client:calls"]).current_dir(&dir).env("CALLS_USERS",r#"{"member":[{"email":"inline@example.test"}]}"#).output().unwrap();
    assert!(child.status.success(),"{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&child.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&child.stderr));let output=String::from_utf8(child.stdout).unwrap();let path=output.lines().next().unwrap();assert!(!std::path::Path::new(path).exists(),"private normalized users cleaned");assert!(output.lines().any(|l|l=="384"),"mode 0600");
    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}
#[test]
fn wave33_001_022_011_021_full_lists_sections_and_repeats_delta() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-guide-{}",std::process::id()));fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|String::from_utf8(Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).output().unwrap().stdout).unwrap();
    for name in ["deploy","production","evolution"] {let text=run(&["guide",name,"--full"]);assert!(text.len()<=8000);assert!(!text.contains("// day-two-production.mjs"));}
    let first=run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working"]);assert!(first.contains("baseline"));let again=run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working"]);assert!(again.contains("new: 0 blocks"));assert!(again.len()<first.len());
    assert_eq!(first,run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working","--repeat"]));
    let failed=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(["guide","deploy","--find","release NO_SUCH_WORD"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(!failed.status.success());assert!(failed.stdout.len()<=8001);
    let opening=run(&["guide","deploy","--section","opening","--repeat"]);assert!(opening.contains("one durable store"));
    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}
#[test]
fn wave33_muse_021_all_merges_retired_cells_and_narrowed_rows_across_retained_releases() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-retained-{}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
    let one="table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20 ?\n  public 'notes'\n  online only\n";
    let two=one.replace("  color: text <=20 ?\n", "").replace("text <=80","text <=4");
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),one).unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|{let result=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).output().unwrap();assert!(result.status.success(),"{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));result};
    run(&["deploy","production"]);
    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),one.into())]);
    let definition=bound.schema.table("notes").unwrap();
    let rows:Vec<_>=[serde_json::json!({"id":"fits","title":"tiny","color":"blue"}),serde_json::json!({"id":"wide","title":"historical wide value","color":"red"})].into_iter().map(|row|("notes".to_owned(),snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(definition,&row).unwrap())).collect();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::open(snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.join("production/store.sqlite")).unwrap()).unwrap();engine.seed(rows).unwrap();drop(engine);
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),&two).unwrap();run(&["deploy","production"]);
    let inspect=|| {let out=run(&["data","notes","--all","--release","production","--json"]);serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&out.stdout).unwrap()};
    let all=inspect();let rows=all["rows"].as_array().unwrap();assert_eq!(rows.len(),2);assert_eq!(rows[0]["title"],"tiny");assert_eq!(rows[0]["color"],"blue");assert_eq!(rows[1]["title"],"historical wide value");assert_eq!(rows[1]["color"],"red");
    run(&["rollback","production","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(inspect()["rows"],all["rows"]);
    run(&["rollback","production","--to","latest"]);assert_eq!(inspect()["rows"],all["rows"]);
    // A later incompatible scalar kind must not turn retained text into null.
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),two.replace("title: text <=4","title: int")).unwrap();
    run(&["deploy","production"]);assert_eq!(inspect()["rows"],all["rows"]);

    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {mkdtempSync,mkdirSync,writeFileSync,readFileSync,rmSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join,resolve} from 'node:path';
import {uniqueName,freshUser,deploy,startServe,cli,snapshotAll,rollback} from '../dist/proof.js';
import {createClient} from '../dist/client.js';
const root=resolve(import.meta.dirname,'../../..'),env={SNAPBACK4_BIN:join(root,'target/debug/snapback4')};
function project(t,source){const cwd=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'dx16-'));t.after(()=>rmSync(cwd,{recursive:true,force:true}));mkdirSync(join(cwd,'snapback'));writeFileSync(join(cwd,'snapback/schema.q'),source);return cwd;}
const schema=`use identity password
table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('member')
  unique principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only
mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('member')):
  return insert roles {principal,role}
table rows:
  owner: principal
  title: text <=80
  cap 1 per owner
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer and exists roles[principal = viewer]
  online only
mutation add(title: text <=80):
  return insert rows {owner:viewer,title}
query list():
  return rows where .owner = viewer first 20 by .id
`;
test('wave33 Muse 011/005/004: two proof runs use fresh users and names without cap collisions',async t=>{
 const cwd=project(t,schema);await deploy('production','snapback',{cwd,env});const host=await startServe('production',{cwd,env});
 try{const principals=new Set(),names=new Set();for(let run=0;run<2;run++){
  const account=await freshUser(host,{role:'member',provision:{name:'provision'}});assert.ok(!principals.has(account.principal));principals.add(account.principal);
  const title=uniqueName('proof');assert.ok(!names.has(title));names.add(title);const client=createClient({url:host.url,session:account});
  try{const write=await client.mutate({name:'add',kind:'mutation'},{title});assert.equal(write.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(write));}finally{client.close();}
 }
 const roleless=await freshUser(host);assert.equal(roleless.role,'users');assert.ok(roleless.token);
 }finally{await host.stop();}
 assert.equal((await snapshotAll('rows',{cwd,env,release:'production'})).length,2);
 assert.throws(()=>uniqueName('../bad'),/E_PROOF_USAGE/);
});
test('wave33 Muse production lookups: bounded digest has helpers and roster/account shapes',()=>{
 const digest=readFileSync(join(root,'crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_proof.md'),'utf8');
 for(const word of ['startServe','provisionAccounts','StagingAccount','freshUser','uniqueName','proof-accounts/default.json','roles'])assert.ok(digest.includes(word),word);
 assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(digest)<=8000);
});

impl Inspection {
    pub fn retained_schema(&self, mut schema: Schema) -> Result<Schema> {
        let conn = &self.conn;

            let table: Table =
                serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| ("E_SCHEMA", e.to_string()))?;
            historical.entry(name).or_insert(table);
        }

            if let Some(table) = schema.tables.get_mut(&name) {
                for (column, kind) in &old.columns {

                        table.columns.entry(column.clone()).or_insert(kind.clone());
                        if let Some(default) = old.defaults.get(column) {

                                .defaults
                                .entry(column.clone())
                                .or_insert(default.clone());

                    }
                }

            .query_map([], |row| {
                Ok((
                    row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                    row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
                    row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
                ))
            })
            .map_err(store_error)?;
        for row in rows {
            let (table, column, definition) = row.map_err(store_error)?;

            if !retained.authoritative {
                continue;
            }

                    .columns
                    .entry(column.clone())
