//! Column admission, maintained fields and authority/identity/delivery dependencies.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#2 — the binder and raw-schema admission share this walk.
use super::*;

pub const GROUP: &str = "$parent";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ColumnOwner {
    pub table: String,
    pub column: String,
}

impl ColumnOwner {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { format!("{}.{}", self.table, self.column) }
    // Bounded labels also keep a first contribution independent of parent state.
    pub fn target(&self) -> String { format!("$column:{}", digest(&(&self.table, &self.column))) }
}

impl Schema {
    pub(crate) fn private_owner(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&ColumnOwner> {
        // Privacy also holds before admission: a duplicate ordinary declaration
        // must never shadow a private one in a raw schema.
        self.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool { self.private_owner(name).is_none() && !self.is_recount_work(name) }

    pub fn visible_tables(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter(|(name, _)| self.visible(name))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — identity remains on Table, which image-retention compares.
    pub fn public_projection(&self) -> Self {
        let mut schema = self.clone();
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.backfills.clear(); schema.downfills.clear(); schema.table_renames.clear(); schema.asset_renames.clear(); schema.extractions.clear(); }
        // Inspection provenance is catalog side metadata, never device input.
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.authored_indexes = Default::default(); }
        schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
        schema.receipt_dependencies.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.source_reservations.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.tables.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.maintains.retain(|m| self.visible(&m.target));
        schema
    }

    pub fn refuse_private(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.is_recount_work(name) { return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_VIEW_KEY",message:"recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()}); }
        if let Some(owner) = self.private_owner(name) {
            return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
                message: format!("{} is maintained by the engine; read the parent row or write the source rows (guide maintains)", owner.label()) });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A projected device schema deliberately has metadata without private maintains.
    /// Authoritative engines must have exactly one derivation for every owned field.
    pub fn validate_column_owners(&self) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        for (name,table) in self.visible_tables() {
            for column in table.maintained.keys() {
                if self.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|&o.table == name && &o.column == column)).count() != 1 {
                    return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name}.{column} needs exactly one engine derivation; declare the column aggregate (guide maintains)")));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Maintain {
    pub fn group_fields(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        if self.private.is_some() { vec![GROUP.into()] } else { self.by.clone() }
    }

    pub fn derivation_identity(&self) -> String { digest(&(&self.kind, &self.source, &self.filter, &self.by)) }
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { self.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| self.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label) }
}

struct Walk<'a> { schema: &'a Schema, issues: Vec<SchemaIssue> }
impl Walk<'_> {
    fn issue(&mut self, path: &str, label: &str) {
        self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: format!("maintained column {label} is depended on by {path}; use an ordinary column for authority, identity, lifetime or delivery, or a separate maintain for an aggregate (guide maintains)") });
    }
    fn target(&mut self, table: &str, path: &str) {
        if self.schema.is_recount_work(table) {self.issues.push(SchemaIssue {path:path.into(),message:"E_VIEW_KEY: recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()});}
        if let Some(owner) = self.schema.private_owner(table) { self.issue(path, &owner.label()); }
    }
    fn field(&mut self, table: &str, field: &str, path: &str) {
        if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, field) {
            self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
        }
        if self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.contains_key(field)) { self.issue(path, &format!("{table}.{field}")); }
    }
    fn term(&mut self, table: &str, term: &RuleTerm, path: &str) {
        if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) = term { self.field(table, field, path); }
    }
    fn index(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, path: &str) {
        self.target(table, path);
        if let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) && let Some(index) = definition.index(index) {
            for field in definition.maintained.keys().filter(|field| index.uses_column(field)) { self.field(table, field, path); }
            for field in index.columns() {
                if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, &field) {
                    self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
                }
            }
        }
    }
    fn rule(&mut self, table: &str, rule: &RuleExpr, path: &str) {
        match rule {
            RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny => {},
            RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { self.term(table,a,path); self.term(table,b,path); },
            RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => for item in items { self.rule(table,item,path); },
            RuleExpr::Not(item) => self.rule(table,item,path),
            RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
                self.index(target,index,path);
                for term in key { self.term(table,term,path); }
                for (field,term) in equalities { self.field(target,field,path); self.term(table,term,path); }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Owned timeline rows are written only on the timeline's scoped transition
/// path, even if their stored ownership sets are forged. Mapped application
/// tables stay ordinary parents and sources: the mirror is not a source write,
/// so it never enters a transition, and delivery copies no parent fields.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#33-f3-the-following-recipes-contract-as-a-declaration — timeline writes require their scoped transition path.
pub fn timeline_issues(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    for m in &schema.maintains {
        let Some(owner) = &m.private else { continue };
        for (role, table) in [("parent", &owner.table), ("source", &m.source)] {
            if let Some(timeline) = schema.timelines.iter().find(|t| t.owns(table)) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{}.columns.{}", owner.table, owner.column),
                    message: format!("maintained column {} has timeline-owned {role} {table} in {}; the timeline writes its own tables, so use a table it maps, such as its posts, or an ordinary table (guide maintains)", owner.label(), timeline.name) });
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — raw catalogs must refuse impossible list ranges too.
fn list_bounds_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::ListRange { of, min, max } => min <= max && list_bounds_valid(of),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } => list_bounds_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — a raw catalog cannot install a range no int satisfies.
fn int_ranges_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::IntRange { min, max } => crate::schema::int_range_valid(*min, *max),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of: inner, .. } => int_ranges_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut walk = Walk { schema, issues: Vec::new() };
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        for (field, kind) in &table.columns {
            if !list_bounds_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "list minimum must not exceed its maximum".into() });
            }
            if !int_ranges_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "an int range needs -(2^53 - 1) <= min <= max <= 2^53 - 1".into() });
            }
        }
    }
    walk.issues.extend(timeline_issues(schema));
    let mut targets = BTreeSet::new();
    for (position, m) in schema.maintains.iter().enumerate() {
        if !targets.insert(&m.target) {
            let label = schema.private_owner(&m.target).map_or_else(|| m.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label);
            walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("maintains[{position}]"),
                message: format!("duplicate maintain target {label}; each target needs exactly one derivation (guide maintains)") });
        }
    }
    for (name, table) in schema.visible_tables() {
        for (field, identity) in &table.maintained {
            if identity.is_empty() || table.defaults.contains_key(field) || table.recorded.contains_key(field)
                || !matches!(table.columns.get(field), Some(ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. })) {
                walk.issue(&format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"), &format!("{name}.{field}"));
            }
        }
        for (effect, rule) in [("read", &table.rules.read), ("insert", &table.rules.insert), ("update", &table.rules.update), ("delete", &table.rules.delete)] {
            walk.rule(name, rule, &format!("tables.{name}.rules.{effect}"));
        }
        for (index_name,index) in &table.indexes {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.indexes.{index_name}");
            if index.unique || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| m.row_key.is_some()) {
                walk.index(name,index_name,&path);
            }
            for component in &index.components {
                if let IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } = component { for field in fields { walk.field(name,field,&path); } }
                if let IndexComponent::Contribution(m) = component {
                    walk.target(&m.target,&path);
                    walk.target(&m.source,&path);
                    for field in m.dependencies() { walk.field(&m.source,&field,&path); }
        if let Some(k)=&m.row_key {for field in k.fields() {walk.field(&k.table,&field,&path);}}
                }
            }
        }
        for constraint in &table.constraints {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.constraint");
            match constraint {
                Constraint::Unique { index } => walk.index(name,index,&path),
                Constraint::Cap { by: fields, .. } | Constraint::Immutable { fields } => for field in fields { walk.field(name,field,&path); },
                Constraint::Text { field, .. } => walk.field(name,field,&path),
                Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert { table: target, index, key } => {
                    walk.index(target,index,&path);
                    for term in key { walk.term(name,term,&path); }
                },
                Constraint::AppendOnly | Constraint::Recorded | Constraint::Capabilities(_) => {},
            }
        }
        if let Some(field) = &table.expire { walk.field(name,field,&format!("tables.{name}.expire")); }
        for field in table.defaults.keys() { walk.field(name,field,&format!("tables.{name}.default")); }
        for (field,capture) in &table.recorded {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.recorded.{field}");
            walk.field(name,&capture.reference,&path);
            walk.target(&capture.table,&path);
            walk.field(&capture.table,&capture.column,&path);
        }
        if let Some(sync) = &table.sync {
            let path = format!("tables.{name}.sync");
            for (audience, horizon) in sync.arms() {
                if let Some(horizon) = horizon { walk.index(name,&horizon.by,&path); }
                match audience {
                    Audience::Public => {},
                    Audience::Column(field) => walk.field(name,field,&path),
                    Audience::Target { table: target, prefix, principal } => {
                        walk.target(target,&path);
                        for field in prefix { walk.field(name,field,&path); walk.field(target,field,&path); }
                        walk.field(target,principal,&path);
                        // Resolved rule probes above carry the target index's actual columns.
                    },
                }
            }
        }
        for (field,kind) in &table.columns {
            if let ColumnType::Ref { table: target } = kind.required() { walk.target(target,&format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}")); }
        }
    }
    for m in &schema.maintains {
        let path = format!("maintain {}",m.label());
        if let Some(rule) = &m.readable { walk.rule(&m.target,rule,&path); }
        if schema.maintain_for(&m.source).is_some() {
            walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.clone(), message: format!("{path} takes generated source {}; use ordinary source rows; aggregate cascades are not supported (guide maintains)", schema.maintain_for(&m.source).unwrap().label()) });
        }
        for field in m.dependencies() { walk.field(&m.source,&field,&path); }
        if let Some(k)=&m.row_key {for field in k.fields() {walk.field(&k.table,&field,&path);}}
        if let Some(owner) = &m.private {
            if schema.maintain_for(&owner.table).is_some() {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path, message: format!("maintained column {} belongs to generated table {}; declare it on an ordinary parent table instead (guide maintains)",owner.label(),owner.table) });
                continue;
            }
            let valid = m.target == owner.target() && m.by.len() == 1 && m.computed.is_empty() && m.readable.is_none()
                && schema.table(&owner.table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.get(&owner.column) == Some(&m.derivation_identity()))
                && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_expiring()
                    && t.columns.get(&m.by[0]).is_some_and(|kind| kind.required() == &ColumnType::Ref { table: owner.table.clone() }))
                && schema.table(&m.target).is_some_and(|t| t.sync.is_none() && t.rules == Rules::deny()
                    && schema.table(&owner.table).is_some_and(|parent|parent.columns.get(&owner.column) == t.columns.get("value")));
            if !valid { walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path, message: format!("{} requires one reference from a non-expiring ordinary source and a private engine-only target; use a separate maintain for expiring sources (guide maintains)",owner.label()) }); }
        }
    }
    walk.issues
}

import { capacityAdvice } from "./proof-fresh.ts";
import { rosterLock } from "./proof-roster-lock.ts";
import { identityRequest } from "./identity-request.ts";
import { randomUUID, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, unlinkSync, lstatSync, fsyncSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import type { ServeHost } from "./proof.ts";

export interface StagingAccount {
  kind: "user"; role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export type ProvisionedAccount = StagingAccount;
export type ProvisionedSessions = StagingSessions;
export interface ProvisionAccountsOptions extends SeedAccountsOptions {
  /** Named persistent roster within this deployment. Default default. */
  roster?: string;
  /** Required for a host not returned by startServe. Keep with the deployment. */
  rosterDir?: string;
}
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles?: Record<string, number>;
  provision?: { readonly name: string };
  /** Additional password users with no provision call; returned under sessions.users. */
  users?: number;
  /** Service seat authorized by the provisioning mutation. Default operator. */
  service?: string;
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {
  // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — custom argument keys override defaults; the seat grants no extra authority.
  const service = options.service ?? "operator";
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles ?? {}), users=options.users ?? 0, timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(users) || users<0 || (!roles.length&&!users) || (users>0&&roles.some(([role])=>role==="users")) || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, users) > 100 || (roles.length>0 || options.provision!==undefined) && (typeof options.provision?.name!=="string" || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision.name))) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  return {service, roles:[...roles,...(users ? [["users",users] as [string,number]] : [])], timeout};
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. Partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard the staging store before retrying. */
async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production" | "development", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}, assertHeld: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const partial = environment === "development" ? "inspect partial development accounts/grants or discard this development store" : environment === "staging" ? "inspect or discard this staging deployment" : "inspect partial production accounts/grants before retrying";
  const remaining = () => {
    assertHeld();

     3	use std::{path::Path, io};
     4	use serde_json::json;
     5	
     6	pub(super) fn files(dir: &Path, sources: &[(String, String)]) -> io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
     7	    if dir.join("v1").symlink_metadata().is_ok() { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
     8	    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
     9	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err(io::Error::other("cannot freeze an unchecked v1 client; fix current schema first")); }
    10	    let mut files = Vec::new();
    11	    fn push(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>, path: String, text: String) {
    12	        if let Some((_,body))=files.iter_mut().find(|(name,_)|*name==path) { *body=text; }
    13	        else { files.push((Box::leak(path.into_boxed_str()),text)); }
    14	    }
    15	    fn walk(root: &Path, relative: &Path, files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>) -> io::Result<()> {
    16	        let path = root.join(relative);
    17	        if !path.exists() { return Ok(()); }
    18	        if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() { return Err(io::Error::other(format!("frozen client refuses symlink {}", path.display()))); }
    19	        if path.is_dir() {
    20	            if matches!(path.file_name().and_then(|n|n.to_str()),Some("node_modules"|"dist"|"target"|".git")) { return Ok(()); }
    21	            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(path)? { walk(root, &relative.join(entry?.file_name()),files)?; }
    22	        } else if matches!(path.extension().and_then(|s|s.to_str()), Some("mjs"|"cjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"|"jsx")) || path.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="package.json") {
    23	            push(files,format!("v1/{}",relative.display()),std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);
    24	        }
    25	        Ok(())
    26	    }
    27	    // Save application modules, not node_modules, stores, credentials or build output.
    28	    for path in ["scripts", "src", "app"] { walk(dir,Path::new(path),&mut files)?; }
    29	    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).into_iter().flatten() {
    30	        let entry=entry?;
    31	        if entry.path().is_file() && entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|e|matches!(e.to_str(),Some("mjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"))) { walk(dir,Path::new(&entry.file_name()),&mut files)?; }
    32	    }
    33	    // Follow literal relative module imports beyond conventional source directories.
    34	    // Runtime-computed paths remain application responsibilities, as in Node itself.
    35	    let canonical_root=dir.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| std::env::current_dir().unwrap_or_default().join(dir));
    36	    let mut index=0;
    37	    while index<files.len() {
    38	        let (saved,text)=&files[index];
    39	        let source=dir.join(saved.strip_prefix("v1/").unwrap());
    40	        let mut imports=Vec::new();
    41	        let bytes=text.as_bytes();let mut cursor=0;
    42	        while cursor<bytes.len() {
    43	            let quote=bytes[cursor];
    44	            if !matches!(quote,b'\''|b'"') {cursor+=1;continue;}
    45	            let start=cursor+1;let mut end=start;
    46	            while end<bytes.len() && bytes[end]!=quote {if bytes[end]==b'\\' {end+=1;}end+=1;}
    47	            if end>=bytes.len() {break;}
    48	            let before=text[..cursor].trim_end();
    49	            let module=before.ends_with("from")||before.ends_with("import")||before.ends_with("import(")||before.ends_with("require(");
    50	            if module {if let Some(value)=text.get(start..end) {if value.starts_with("./")||value.starts_with("../") {imports.push(value.to_owned());}}}
    51	            cursor=end+1;
    52	        }
    53	        for import in imports {
    54	            let path=source.parent().unwrap().join(import);
    55	            if !path.exists() {continue;}
    56	            let canonical=path.canonicalize()?;
    57	            let relative=canonical.strip_prefix(&canonical_root).map_err(|_|io::Error::other("frozen relative import escapes the app; keep client modules inside the project"))?;
    58	            let saved=format!("v1/{}",relative.display());
    59	            if files.iter().any(|(name,_)|*name==saved) {continue;}
    60	            if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() {return Err(io::Error::other("frozen module imports must not use symlinks"));}
    61	            if path.is_file() {push(&mut files,saved,std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);}
    62	        }
    63	        index+=1;
    64	    }
    65	    for (name,text) in sources { push(&mut files,format!("v1/snapback/{name}"),text.clone()); }
    66	    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/api.ts".into(),snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound));
    67	    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/package.json".into(),"{\"type\":\"module\"}\n".into());
    68	    let package: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
    69	    let command=package["scripts"]["client:calls"].as_str();
    70	    let mut frozen=json!({"private":true,"type":"module","scripts":{"client:calls":"node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs"}});
    71	    if package["scripts"].is_object() { frozen["scripts"]=package["scripts"].clone(); }
    72	    frozen["scripts"]["client:calls"]=json!("node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs");
    73	    if let Some(command)=command { frozen["scripts"]["client:calls:frozen"]=json!(command); }
    74	    for group in ["dependencies","devDependencies"] { if package[group].is_object() { frozen[group]=package[group].clone(); } }
    75	    push(&mut files,"v1/package.json".into(),super::pretty(&frozen));
    76	    push(&mut files,"v1/.snapback4-frozen-run.mjs".into(),include_str!("frozen-run.mjs").into());
    77	    Ok(files)
    78	}
# Day two: change, prove, release, return

Start here with an existing app. Keep its store and saved sources. Before editing:

```sh
npx snapback4 init --adapt --day2 --apply
npx snapback4 deploy ./deploy --save-version versions/v1
```

Adapt saves the current sources/fixtures in versions/v1 and versions/v2 if absent,
and a frozen v1/ client with its own ESM package and generated API. Existing
snapshots and application-owned proofs stay untouched. Run this before the v2
edit; a snapshot taken afterwards is not historical evidence. If v1 is already
saved/deployed, inspect `releases ./deploy --json`; reuse that saved source.

Read only this card, ONE change route below, and `guide api proof` when writing
proofs. Do not read deploy/production/evolution --full next: those commands list
reference sections. Do not read dist/proof.js or the full declarations. Use the
signature digest and a specific section only when a refusal needs it.

## Choose one change route

| Change | Read at most these sections |
|---|---|
| Rename a field/table/operation or argument | `guide rename --section opening`; `guide deploy --section 'Keep v1 clients working'` |
| Loosen a required field, allow null or an empty album | `guide deploy --section 'Bridge a safety gate'`; `guide security --section 'N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING'` if the check asks for presence intent |
| Add a required field computed from old values | `guide deploy --section 'Migrations and adoption'`; `guide deploy --section 'Which migration shape'` |
| Change record kind, move media into children, split a list | `guide evolution --section 'Move media'`; `guide deploy --section 'Keep v1 clients working'` |

For a spelling change use `renamed from`; retain old endpoint/argument aliases
and result wrappers for v1 callers. Backfill computes historical values; it
isn't maintenance on later edits. Guard every creating/updating path that can
change a derived field. New required columns need a literal default or named
backfill; `default now`/`viewer` cannot invent history. Media/reference moves
need the supported extract shape, not a scalar transform. A loosened type does
not authorize an absent value at every workflow transition: keep independent
presence intent and current guards. Answer the actual check question; a
presence tautology is no safety proof.

## Preview, deploy v2, rollback and forward

Update your working snapback/*.q and versions/v2 (source and contemporary
fixtures); keep versions/v1 frozen. With production v1 already in ./deploy:

```sh
npx snapback4 check
npx snapback4 test --source versions/v2
npx snapback4 deploy ./deploy --schema versions/v2 --check
npx snapback4 deploy ./deploy --schema versions/v2
npx snapback4 rollback ./deploy --to 1 --check
npx snapback4 rollback ./deploy --to 1
npx snapback4 rollback ./deploy --to latest --check
npx snapback4 rollback ./deploy --to latest
```

Replace 1 with the saved v1 release ID. Preview changes no live state and later
writes can change its result. Await activation; no sleep is needed. Default
rollback may use a registered compatible view retaining current guards. Inspect
`releases ./deploy --json` for live source/view and row shape; a successful
rollback alone does not establish frozen-v1 result compatibility. Use --exact
only after deliberately reviewing the old source's broader policy. If preview
requires a bridge or --restore-dormant, follow its concrete instruction and
preview again. Never prune/reset/delete storage to make a proof pass.

Run `npm run day2:proof` twice. The generated proof owns a fresh temporary store,
checks persisted IDs/values across two rollback/forward cycles and cleans up.
Its default business hook proves storage conservation only: add authenticated
v1/v2 business calls, refusals and reads of v2-native rows. Return explicit
changed-field/deletion declarations for intentional old-client writes. A custom
proof must also preserve data and stop every host it starts.

## Frozen v1 calls and repeatable users

```sh
BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4400 CALLS_USERS='{"member":[{"email":"...","password":"..."}]}' npm --prefix v1 run client:calls
```

The frozen runner accepts inline JSON or a JSON file path, normalizes inline
users to a private temporary file for inherited clients, and removes it after
exit. It keeps the frozen generated handles. Run it on v1, live v2, rollback
and forward; use BACKEND_URL from your own bounded serve proof. Missing day-one
client:calls is explicit: author that business client before claiming coverage.
The frozen script may intentionally fail a newly forbidden call; record the
code separately from transport failures. Do not replace its API with v2's.

```js
import { uniqueName, freshUser } from "snapback4/proof";
const title = uniqueName("proof");
const member = await freshUser(host, {role:"member", provision:api.provision});
// member has email/password/principal/token; freshUser uses real signup and
// the ordinary operator provision mutation. Customize args for your schema.
```

Use freshUser once per role per proof run and reuse its session across that
run's release cycles. uniqueName supplies fresh row names/intent IDs; it cannot
clear a per-owner cap. A cap refusal on a second run needs a fresh owner/group
or deliberate authorized cleanup. Never raise caps or delete the shared
production store just to get green. Capacity refusals remain failures with a
setup hint, never silently retried writes.

## Production roster and complete inspection

`guide api proof` includes startServe, provisionAccounts, account/roster shapes
and exact options. For a persistent production driver use
`guide production --section 'Serve script and reusable accounts'` only. Signup
creates an account; an authorized provisioning mutation grants its role.

```js
import { startServe } from "snapback4/proof";
const host = await startServe("./deploy", {
  provisionUsers:{roles:{member:2, admin:2}, provision:api.provision}
});
const users = host.sessions; // {member:[account,account], admin:[account,account]}
// Private harness readiness: {backendUrl:host.url, users}; do not log elsewhere.
// Stop host in finally, or use your existing serve-check bounded driver.
```

Production provisioning reuses the named roster and logs in on rerun. Keep
production/proof-accounts/default.json (0600) with its store; it holds credentials,
stable provision IDs and partial completion. Use freshUser for isolated test
batches, the persistent roster for production accounts. Neither bypasses rules.

`data TABLE --release ./deploy --all --json` and
`snapshotAll("TABLE", {release:"./deploy"})` include dormant cells and retained
rows, merged once per ID. Active values win; deleted rows stay absent. This is
administrative inspection, bypassing application read rules, not a client
compatibility proof. Ephemeral rows exist only in serving memory. Inspect
`releases ./deploy --json` separately for retained source history.

export interface StagingAccount {
  kind: "user"; role: string; email: string; password: string; principal: string; token: string; expiresAt: number;
}
export type StagingSessions = Record<string, StagingAccount[]>;
export type ProvisionedAccount = StagingAccount;
export type ProvisionedSessions = StagingSessions;
export interface ProvisionAccountsOptions extends SeedAccountsOptions {
  /** Named persistent roster within this deployment. Default default. */
  roster?: string;
  /** Required for a host not returned by startServe. Keep with the deployment. */
  rosterDir?: string;
}
export interface SeedAccountsOptions {
  roles?: Record<string, number>;
  provision?: { readonly name: string };
  /** Additional password users with no provision call; returned under sessions.users. */
  users?: number;
  /** Service seat authorized by the provisioning mutation. Default operator. */
  service?: string;
  args?: (account: StagingAccount) => Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Whole setup deadline, including rate-limit waits. Default 5 minutes. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {
  // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — custom argument keys override defaults; the seat grants no extra authority.
  const service = options.service ?? "operator";
  const roles = Object.entries(options.roles ?? {}), users=options.users ?? 0, timeout = options.timeoutMs ?? 300_000;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(users) || users<0 || (!roles.length&&!users) || (users>0&&roles.some(([role])=>role==="users")) || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)
      || roles.reduce((sum, [, n]) => sum + n, users) > 100 || (roles.length>0 || options.provision!==undefined) && (typeof options.provision?.name!=="string" || !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/.test(options.provision.name))) {
    throw Error("E_STAGING_INPUT: provide 1..100 accounts across named roles, a mutation handle and a positive timeoutMs");
  }
  return {service, roles:[...roles,...(users ? [["users",users] as [string,number]] : [])], timeout};
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — real credentials and ordinary operator mutations, never system seeding or auth bypass.
/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. Partial accounts/grants may exist; inspect or discard the staging store before retrying. */
async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production" | "development", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}, assertHeld: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);
  const end = performance.now() + timeout;
  const partial = environment === "development" ? "inspect partial development accounts/grants or discard this development store" : environment === "staging" ? "inspect or discard this staging deployment" : "inspect partial production accounts/grants before retrying";
  const remaining = () => {
    assertHeld();
    const ms = Math.ceil(end - performance.now());
    if (ms <= 0) throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: setup incomplete; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
    return ms;
  };

#[test]
fn untouched_wave9_screens_still_adapt_and_edited_clients_stay_owned() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        for password in [false, true] {
            let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w10-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap(); std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
            let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q").replace("use identity ", "use identity password ");
            std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
            let (app, client) = starter::previous_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
            let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
            std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), &app).unwrap(); std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?}, password={password}: previous screens recognized");
            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path), "edited pair stays owned");
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
        }
    }
}

/// Paragraphs and complete fences are the units of reading and project deltas.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#8 — never cut a runnable fence in half.
pub fn reading_blocks(text: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut blocks = Vec::new(); let mut block = String::new(); let mut fenced = false;
    for line in text.lines() {
        if line.trim_start().starts_with("```") { fenced = !fenced; }
        if line.is_empty() && !fenced {
            if !block.is_empty() { blocks.push(std::mem::take(&mut block)); }
        } else { block.push_str(line); block.push('\n'); }
    }
    if !block.is_empty() { blocks.push(block); } blocks
}

/// Conservative byte-token ceiling: any UTF-8 byte needs at most one byte token.
pub fn bounded_default(name: &str, text: &str) -> String {
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#8-wave-32-builder-reading-and-waiting-tok1 — signatures come from built declarations, never handwritten API copies.
import ts from 'typescript';
import { getEncoding } from 'js-tiktoken';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
const root = new URL('../', import.meta.url), dist = new URL('dist/', root);
const out = new URL('../../crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/', root);
const encoding = getEncoding('o200k_base');
const modules = {
  proof: ['startDev','startServe','ServeOptions','ProofOptions','ProofHost','serveUntilSignal','probeServeGuard','deploy','cli','command','startStatic','withBrowser','renderedText','labelledControl','waitForFresh','exhaustPages','snapshotAll','serveCheck','ServeCheckOptions','provisionAccounts','ProvisionAccountsOptions','SeedAccountsOptions','StagingSessions','DevHost','ServeHost','CliResult','ProvisionedSessions','StagingAccount','uniqueName','freshUser','FreshUserOptions'],
  client: ['createClient','ClientOptions','Client','Observed','ObserveOptions','Read','Write','Signal','Cursor','AssetRead','Uploaded','settleMutation','Session','SignIn','Query','Mutation','MutateOptions','mintId','idFrom','Ready','SettledRead','Refusal','MutationPromise','UploadOptions'],
  auth: ['signup','login','SignIn','Session','SignInOptions','guest','SessionStore','memorySessionStore','restoreSession','keepSession','forgetSession','logout','logoutAll','refreshSession','narrowSession'],
};
function digest(module, names) {
  const file = fileURLToPath(new URL(`${module}.d.ts`, dist));
  const program = ts.createProgram([file], { noResolve: false, skipLibCheck: true, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ESNext, module: ts.ModuleKind.NodeNext, moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.NodeNext });
  const checker = program.getTypeChecker(), source = program.getSourceFile(file);
  const exports = checker.getExportsOfModule(checker.getSymbolAtLocation(source));
  const hashes = new Map();
  function declarations(name, compact = true) {
    let symbol = exports.find(e => e.name === name);
    if (!symbol) {
      const dependency = ts.createSourceFile('contract.d.ts', readFileSync(new URL('contract.d.ts',dist),'utf8'), ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
      const node = dependency.statements.find(s => s.name?.text === name);
      if (!node) throw Error(`guide api ${module}: type ${name} missing`);
      hashes.set('contract.d.ts', createHash('sha256').update(dependency.text).digest('hex'));
      return `// ${name}: import type from snapback4/contract\n` + node.getText(dependency).replace(/\/\*\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
    }
    if (symbol.flags & ts.SymbolFlags.Alias) symbol = checker.getAliasedSymbol(symbol);
    return (symbol.declarations ?? []).map(node => {
      const source = node.getSourceFile();
      hashes.set(source.fileName.split('/').at(-1), createHash('sha256').update(source.text).digest('hex'));
      let text = (ts.isVariableDeclaration(node) ? 'export const ' : '') + node.getText(source);
      // Client's core methods replace the much larger advanced-lifecycle surface.
      if (compact && name === 'Client' && ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node)) {
        const wanted = ['query','mutate','observe','upload','asset','close','watch'];
        const members = node.members.filter(n => wanted.includes(n.name?.getText(source)));
        text = `export interface Client { ${members.map(n => n.getText(source)).join(' ')} /* other methods: dist/contract.d.ts */ }`;
      }
      return text.replace(/\/\*\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/^export declare /, 'export ');
    }).join('\n');
  }
  const pieces = names.map(name => ({ name, text: declarations(name) }));
  let title = `# API ${module}: exact signature digest\n\nImport from "snapback4/${module}". Generated from dist declarations; types named below retain their declaration meaning.\n`;
  if (module === 'client') title += 'observe returns getSnapshot/subscribe/close; query returns Read, mutate returns Write; asset returns a URL handle or denied. Upload each image column separately. sentResult is from snapback4/react or contract.\n';
  if (module === 'proof') title += 'Production: startServe(dir,{provisionUsers:{roles:{member:2,admin:2},provision:api.provision}}) returns host.sessions, shaped {member:[account,account],admin:[account,account]}. Account = {kind,role,email,password,principal,token,expiresAt}. Signup grants no role. The private persistent roster is production/proof-accounts/default.json (0600): {version:1,plan,entries:[{role,index,email,password,id,done,principal?,args?}]}; keep it with its store. provisionAccounts reuses this roster; freshUser creates an isolated proof identity instead. Read guide production --section "Serve script and reusable accounts" for partial-setup recovery; never read dist/proof.js.\n';
  if (module === 'proof') title += 'Use probe:check for verification; probe:serve stays up until SIGTERM (deliberate harness run: --hold).\n';
  const render = entries => `${title}\n\`\`\`ts\n${entries.map(p => p.text).join('\n')}\n\`\`\`\n\nOther exports/options: dist/${module}.d.ts; follow its named imports only on demand.\n`;
  const selected = [];
  for (const piece of pieces) { if (encoding.encode(render([...selected,piece])).length <= 2800) selected.push(piece); }
  const omitted = pieces.filter(p => !selected.includes(p)).map(p => p.name);
  let text = render(selected);
  if (omitted.length) text += `Additional requested declarations in dist/${module}.d.ts: ${omitted.join(', ')}.\n`;
  // A source digest covers selected and omitted requested declarations, so drift fails.
  text += `\nDeclaration fingerprint: ${createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify([...hashes].sort())).digest('hex')}\n`;
  if (encoding.encode(text).length > 3000) throw Error(`guide api ${module} exceeds 3000 tokens`);
  if (module !== 'proof') {
    const complete = `${title}\n\`\`\`ts\n${exports.map(s => declarations(s.name, false)).join('\n')}\n\`\`\`\n`;
    for (const target of [new URL(`api_${module}_full.md`,out), new URL(`guide-api/${module}-full.md`,dist)]) {
      if (process.argv.includes('--check')) { if (readFileSync(target,'utf8') !== complete) throw Error(`guide api ${module} --full drift`); }
      else writeFileSync(target, complete);
    }
  }
  return text;
}
for (const [module,names] of Object.entries(modules)) {
  const text = digest(module,names), tracked = new URL(`api_${module}.md`,out), packed = new URL(`guide-api/${module}.md`,dist);
  mkdirSync(new URL('guide-api/',dist), { recursive:true });
  if (process.argv.includes('--check')) { if (readFileSync(tracked,'utf8') !== text || readFileSync(packed,'utf8') !== text) throw Error(`guide api ${module} drift; rebuild client`); }
  else { writeFileSync(tracked,text); writeFileSync(packed,text); }
  console.log(`guide api ${module}: ${encoding.encode(text).length}/3000 tokens`);
}

//! Declaration digests are generated by the client build; no manually copied signatures.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#8-wave-32-builder-reading-and-waiting-tok1 — on-demand module shapes replace d.ts greps.
pub fn render(module: &str, full: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
    match module {
        "proof" if full => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/proof_api_reference.md").into()),
        "proof" => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_proof.md").into()),
        "client" if full => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_client_full.md").into()),
        "client" => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_client.md").into()),
        "auth" if full => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_auth_full.md").into()),
        "auth" => Ok(include_str!("../../snapback4-lang/src/guide/api_auth.md").into()),
        "generated" => {
            let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let root = cwd.ancestors().find(|p| p.join("snapback").is_dir()).ok_or("guide api generated: run in your project after snapback4 check")?;
            let api = std::fs::read_to_string(root.join("snapback/generated/api.ts")).map_err(|_| "guide api generated: run snapback4 check first")?;
            let index = api.lines().skip(1).take_while(|l| !l.starts_with("// Full signatures")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
            if !index.contains("Operation index") { return Err("guide api generated: regenerate api.ts with snapback4 check".into()); }
            Ok(format!("# Your checked API\n\n{index}\n\nImport api from ./snapback/generated/api.ts. Use api.<operation> with its named argument object. Full ArgsOf/ResultOf and row types live below that file's index.\n"))
        }
        _ => Err("usage: snapback4 guide api <proof|client|auth|generated>".into()),
    }
}

pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
    super::extractions::delete(tx,t,id)?;
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    tx.conn().execute(
        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
        params![t, id],
    )?;
    Ok(())
//! Plain inspection records and catalog access shared by inspection policy.
// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#32-the-rule — only adapters touch inspection connections.
pub type InspectionResult<T> = Result<T, (&'static str, String)>;
pub const PRIVATE: &str = "_snapback_sql_";
pub const CATALOG_HINT: &str = "`\\dt` lists tables; `\\d messages` describes one";

// P1: implement catalog inspection for the Postgres read-only role.
pub trait InspectionCatalog {
    fn active_schema_record(&self) -> InspectionResult<(i64, String)>;
    fn inspection_row_count(&self, name: &str, limit: u64) -> InspectionResult<u64>;
    fn inspection_provenance(&self) -> InspectionResult<Option<(i64, String)>>;
    fn inspection_adopted(&self) -> InspectionResult<bool>;
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub enum WorkRecordKind {
    Action,
    Mutation,
    Effect,
    ActionStep,
}
pub struct HandlerFailure {
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub state: String,
    pub attempts: i64,
    pub error: String,
}
pub struct CommitClockRecord {
    pub at: i64,
    pub touched: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub length: Option<i64>,
}

/// Shared visibility policy for generated inspection views and catalog output.
pub fn internal(name: &str) -> bool {
    name.starts_with('_')
        || matches!(name.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(), "r" | "i")
        || name.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("sqlite_")
}

/// Interpret an unclassified query failure above the physical adapter.
pub fn query_error(schema: &crate::schema::Schema, message: String) -> (&'static str, String) {
    // SQLite identifiers are case-insensitive, including diagnostic spellings.
    let folded = message.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if let Some(m) = schema
        .maintains
        .iter()
        .find(|m| m.private.is_some() && folded.contains(&m.target.to_ascii_lowercase()))
    {
        (
            "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
            format!(
                "{} is engine-maintained; read its parent column (guide maintains)",
                m.label()
            ),
        )
    } else {
        ("E_INPUT", format!("SQL: {message}"))
    }
}

/// Object rows must retain every output column at both preparation points.
pub fn check_output_names(columns: &[String], object_rows: bool) -> InspectionResult<()> {
    if object_rows {
        let mut names = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        if columns
            .iter()
            .any(|name| !names.insert(name.to_ascii_lowercase()))
        {
            return Err((
                "E_INPUT",
                "--json-objects needs unique output names; alias duplicate columns".into(),
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

**MATERIAL**

Two correctness problems in frozen-client capture:

1. **The actual `client:calls` entry can be omitted.** [frozen.rs:28](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/init/frozen.rs:28) seeds capture from `scripts/`, `src/`, `app/` and root modules. A valid script such as `"client:calls":"node tools/calls.mjs"` is preserved in the frozen package, but `tools/calls.mjs` is never captured unless another captured module imports it. Adapt succeeds; the frozen command fails with a missing module.

   **Fix:** seed capture from the configured command’s application entry, then follow its dependencies. If the command cannot be captured, report that gap explicitly before applying.

2. **Comments can silently defeat dependency discovery.** [frozen.rs:42](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/init/frozen.rs:42) treats every quote as a string delimiter, including apostrophes in comments. For example:
   ```js
   // Don't edit.
   import { check } from '../helpers/check.mjs';
   ```
   The apostrophe consumes the import’s opening quote, so the dependency is missed. With `helpers/` outside the initial directories, capture succeeds but the frozen client cannot execute.

   **Fix:** use syntax-aware import discovery that skips comments and handles literal static imports, re-exports, dynamic imports and requires. Add regressions for both cases.

**Optional improvements:** none raised.

Reviewed §9 and the implementation delta at `125e9f9481` from `c9c3a35461`. No files edited, builds or tests run, or reviewer/author reasoning read; no test results assumed.
